# Snorlax-Bot v0.1 spec — chat layout + agent messaging

Product: Snorlax-Bot
Repo: https://github.com/chinghauchu/snorlax-bot
Audience: Backend, Frontend (macOS Tauri), iOS
Status: **locked 2026-08-25**. Engineering may build from this. CEO confirmed the four defaults. Backend's hop 0→1 / one-mention cut is **rejected**.

**v0.1.1 (CEO 2026-08-25, after Chinghau's test):** 1:1 transcripts are isolated. Agent–agent traffic displays in the seeded `Snorlax-Bot` group channel, not in 1:1s. This overrides the v0.1 "DM-in-1:1s / involve bubble" surface. Hop caps and runtime-routed mentions are unchanged. See §2, §4, and §7.

**v0.2 (CEO 2026-08-25):** Collaboration fluency. A user `@chip` in A's 1:1, or A DMing B, opens a **channel thread** under a LEFT `kind=handoff` card authored as A. Pack B wakes with `{ originating, userAsk, brief, mentionedIds }` (not a quote). A's 1:1 stays user + A + a jump chip when the user caused the handoff. Isolation is unchanged. See §8.

**v0.3 (CEO 2026-08-25):** Agent identity + info pane. Header click (desktop also Cmd+Shift+I) opens a 320px overlay / iOS sheet. `kind=agent` is editable identity; `kind=channel` is a read-only member list. Seed DELETE is 204 with no auto-reseed. Isolation is unchanged. See §9.

**v0.4 (CEO 2026-08-26, after Chinghau's 1:1 @mention screenshot):** Collaboration report-back + extra user-created channels. 1:1 @involves log on seed `snorlax-bot-group` when present. B does the task in the channel thread (no ACK classifier). A reports each result in A's 1:1 as that peer lands (not a hop). Isolation is unchanged: B does not appear as B in A's 1:1. Users can create additional `kind=channel` rows and PATCH their members. See §10.

**CEO override 2026-08-26:** seed channel `snorlax-bot-group` DELETE is **204**, gone from GET, **no auto-reseed** — never recreate `snorlax-bot-group`. Empty roster is OK (no agents and/or no channels) and keeps chrome. After seed channel delete, if anything remains in the roster, select it — **agent first**, else a remaining channel. Sidebar shows Delete on the Snorlax-Bot **row** (right-click / iOS swipe), not in the info pane. No jump chip if no channel remains. New agents auto-join `snorlax-bot-group` only if that row still exists; if the seed is gone they join nothing. Seed channel PATCH stays 409. 1:1 @mention report-back still works with zero channels (B does the ask, not written to B’s 1:1; A answers in A’s 1:1 as A, non-hop, `senderId=A`, no `from` field). A2A log: if `snorlax-bot-group` exists, always log there (ignore body `channelId`). Else if the 1:1 POST body has `channelId` and it is an existing `kind=channel` row, log there. Else skip (no `handoff` field, no jump chip; still run B and A’s report-back). Unknown/stale `channelId` after seed delete: skip the log, do not 422, do not recreate seed.

**v0.4 (CEO 2026-08-26, after Chinghau's 1:1 @mention screenshot):** Collaboration report-back + extra user-created channels. 1:1 @involves log on seed `snorlax-bot-group` only. B does the task in the channel thread (no ACK classifier). A reports each result in A's 1:1 as that peer lands (not a hop). Isolation is unchanged: B does not appear as B in A's 1:1. Users can create additional `kind=channel` rows and PATCH their members. See §10.

**v0.5 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Grok-Bot-like basic tools. Runtime-owned function-calling loop (files, shell, web) in per-agent and per-channel sandboxes under `~/.snorlax-bot` (not a Mac folder picker). Shell has no extra network; HTTP is `web_search` / `web_fetch` only. Tools auto-run (no approval/question widgets). Search provider is env/config, not a hardcoded vendor. No MCP, no computer GUI / live preview. See §11.

**v0.6 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Thin Computer pane over the **existing** `~/.snorlax-bot` workspace. Desktop: 320px **right pane** next to chat (same width language as the identity overlay, but this is a column, not a 320px overlay). Collapsible; default open. iOS: no computer pane this slice (chat + muted tool traces only). Same roots as runtime `workspace_for()`. Clients never call tools or the model; new GETs are runtime reads of the sandbox. No terminal GUI, no screenshot stream, no take-over vs watch-only, no MCP, no question widgets, no Mac folder picker. See §12.

**v0.7 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Runtime MCP client. FastAPI is the MCP client (stdio subprocess + LAN HTTP/SSE). Desktop/iOS never speak MCP. Config is `mcp.json` under `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR` (default `~/.snorlax-bot`), not a desktop/iOS bundle or Settings picker. Empty/missing file = no MCP; built-ins still work. MCP tools join the existing runtime-owned OpenAI loop (auto-run, max 8, `kind=tool` persist + `tool.start`/`tool.done`). Built-in names win; MCP tools are namespaced `server__tool`. Shell still has no extra network. MCP HTTP is the runtime’s HTTP path (like `web_fetch`). Failed MCP server start does not block boot. Failed MCP tool still leaves a normal assistant follow-up (#34). LAN URLs may be loopback, RFC1918, or `.local`; reject `169.254.169.254`. See §13.

**v0.8 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Question widgets. Runtime-owned `ask_user_question` ends that agent turn. Persist `kind=widget` LEFT (same speaker as the agent) with `{ prompt, helpText?, options[1-6], allowCustom?, multiSelect?, dismissOnMoveOn? }` plus `widgetStatus` pending|resolved|dismissed and `widgetValues` on that same Message. SSE: persist, then `message.done` with the full Message, then the stream ends. No `widget.*` event. Answer is POST `{ widgetReply: { id, values?, dismissed? } }` — not a user-right bubble and not a new user Message. Dismiss does not wake the agent. A pick continues that POST as hop-0. One pending widget per transcript (409 if the model emits a second, or if the user POSTs content while pending unless `dismissOnMoveOn`). Isolation and thread-only channel widgets stand. Tools stay auto-run (no approval widget). See §14.

**v0.9 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Skills and cron routines. One routine = one agent + one SKILL.md slug. Skills load from `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR/skills/<slug>/SKILL.md`. Scheduler is runtime-owned; cron is 5-field in Asia/Taipei (fixed zone, not the Mac TZ). A due run writes a normal assistant Message in that agent's 1:1 (`senderId=A`, `kind=message`, optional `routineName` kicker). Not a hop, not a widget, not a channel post. Isolation stands: B never appears in A's 1:1. `GET /v1/agents/{id}/routines` lists `{ id, name, skill, schedule, enabled }` (404 missing agent; **409** channel). Pause/enable is `PATCH .../routines/{rid}` `{ enabled }` (404 unknown; **409** channel). POST seeds `{ name, skill, schedule }` (201; **422** unknown skill / bad cron / channel). No DELETE. Do not fire while paused. Missed ticks while the runtime was down are skipped (no catch-up storm). Chrome is list + enable/pause only on the agent info pane. Widgets OK if a run needs a decision (reuse v0.8). Seed channel DELETE 204 stands. OpenAPI **0.9.0**. See §15.

**v0.10 (CEO 2026-08-26):** MCP connect chrome. Runtime owns MCP (v0.7 `mcp.json` stdio + LAN). Clients never speak MCP or the model. `GET /v1/plugins` → `{ id, name, status: connected|needsAuth }` (bare array; catalog may be empty). Connect tap: `POST /v1/plugins/{id}/auth` → `{ authorizationUrl }` for the OS browser (desktop system browser; iOS `ASWebAuthenticationSession`); OAuth callback hits the runtime. No uninstall / delete / store / search API. No Add-custom UI (hand-edited `mcp.json` still works). Chat card is Message `kind=connect` (widget chrome family, **not** `kind=widget`). Same-row reply `connectReply { id }` or `{ dismissed: true }` — no user-right bubble. Isolation matches widgets. After Connected, tools auto-run. Plugins list is **Settings only** (runtime-global), not the agent pane. OpenAPI **0.10.0**. Isolation, report-back, widgets, cron, seed DELETE 204, Mac oMLX, no Mac folder picker stand. See §16.

**v0.11 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Assistant markdown. Clients render only. `Message.content` stays a **string**. Do not add `contentType`, mime, html, or `blocks[]`. Runtime does not rewrite assistant text to HTML or split one message into many. SSE `message.delta` is still text chunks of that same string. User messages stay plain text as stored. Keep widget / connect / tool / routine fields. No MCP mix-in. Assistant LEFT `kind=message` is **14px markdown** with **no grey agent bubble**, **16/14** headings, lists, bold. User-right stays **plain text** (`https://` tappable). Fenced code: **full turn width**, **8px radius**, elevated fill + 1px border, **12px muted language + Copy**, **12px/1.45** mono, horizontal scroll. `https://` links: accent, hover underline, OS browser. Inline code: **13px** mono, **4px** radius, accent 18% mix. Tool / widget / Connect are not markdown. Out of scope: mermaid, math/KaTeX, raw HTML (escape/strip; never inject HTML from model text). OpenAPI **0.11.0**. Isolation / report-back / seed DELETE 204 / Mac oMLX / no Mac folder picker / `workspaceTree.ts` (never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`) stand. See §17.

**v0.12 (CEO 2026-08-26):** MCP Add custom. Runtime owns MCP (v0.7 `mcp.json` stdio + LAN). Clients never speak MCP or the model. Settings Plugins (already Settings-only, runtime-global): **Add** sheet (320px on Settings; name + segmented `stdio` / `URL`) submits `POST /v1/plugins` `{ name, transport: "stdio" | "url", command?, args?, url? }` → **201** `{ id, name, status }`. **Remove** is `DELETE /v1/plugins/{id}` (204; uninstall plus disconnect; unknown 404). No separate disconnect endpoint. Do not auto-open a connect card. GET and `POST .../auth` stay as v0.10. `kind=connect` cards unchanged. No store, no search catalog. OpenAPI **0.12.0**. Isolation, report-back, widgets, markdown, cron, seed DELETE 204, Mac oMLX, no Mac folder picker, `workspaceTree.ts` (never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`) stand. See §18.

**v0.13 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Event listeners, webhook first. Runtime-owned. A routine has **either cron or a trigger, never both**. Result still fires as that agent's 1:1 LEFT `kind=message` as A, with `routineName` kicker (same as v0.9 cron). Isolation + report-back stand. B never paints in A's 1:1. Channel thread only if that's how cron already works — keep existing 1:1-as-A behavior. Every event-trigger routine has a URL + **Copy** in the agent identity pane row. An HTTP **POST** to that URL fires the routine (same SKILL.md / prompt path as cron). Slack/GitHub listeners only if that MCP plugin is **already connected**. Do **not** block this slice on Slack/GitHub. No new OAuth / connect chrome. If a plugin isn't connected, omit those trigger types or 422 — webhook must still work with zero plugins. Chrome: muted line shows the trigger (`Webhook` / `Weekdays 9:00`). Pause still works (PATCH enabled). **No New routine button** in chrome if v0.9 still has no create UI — seed/API-created routines are enough; pane gets Copy for webhook URL. Out: full Box/VNC, teach-a-task, marketplace. Desktop first, iOS match. OpenAPI **0.13.0**. See §19.

**v0.14 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Box computer preview. Runtime owns a **per-agent sandbox display** (1280×800). Clients never send clicks this slice. Clients never speak MCP or the model. `GET /v1/agents/{id}/computer` → `{ hasSandbox, width: 1280, height: 800, imageUrl }`. `imageUrl` is **Bearer PNG** at `GET /v1/agents/{id}/computer/screenshot` (same SNORLAX_TOKEN). Channel id → **409**. Missing agent 404. **No** click/key/scroll POST. File-tree workspace GETs stay as they are (v0.6 / `workspaceTree.ts`). Do **not** reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Do **not** change the existing 320px file-tree column. Chrome is the **agent identity pane only** (desktop 320px overlay / iOS sheet), **above Routines**: 12px muted `Computer`, then a **16:10** shot (**288×180**, **8px** radius, **1px `--border`**). Letterbox the 1280×800 sandbox into that. Display only: default cursor, **no overlay, no Open**, no clicks. Live: poll GET computer / screenshot ~1–2s while the pane is open. No sandbox: omit `imageUrl`, **no frame**, 12px muted `No computer yet.` Otherwise the desktop is **always on**, even idle. iOS: same block, 16:10, 8pt radius, 1pt border, 12pt labels. **No tap-to-open.** Channel pane unchanged. Teach-a-task waits. OpenAPI **0.14.0**. See §20.

**v0.16 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Teach-a-task. **Desktop + runtime only.** iOS still is unchanged this slice — no Record, no Save-as-skill, no `ios/` UI (generated `/v1` types only if the OpenAPI bump requires). Spec rides this PR. OpenAPI **0.16.0**. **No second PR.** Record only exists **inside a desktop takeover session** (the v0.15 Open overlay). Preview `Open` and iOS still are unchanged. Out: blank New skill UI, edit generated markdown, marketplace, iOS record, VNC, a separate OS window. See §22.

**v0.17 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Create/delete routine chrome. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. OpenAPI **0.17.0**. **No second PR.** Agent identity pane **Routines** header gets trailing **12px `Add`** (same family as Plugins `Add`; iOS 12pt). Add sheet (same family as Add plugin / Save as skill, **320px**). Out of scope: marketplace catalog, iOS Open/Record/takeover, skill markdown editor, Slack/GitHub trigger builder. See §23.

**v0.18 (CEO 2026-08-26):** Skill markdown editor. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. OpenAPI **0.18.0**. **No second PR.** Agent identity pane **Skills** block **below Routines**. Computer / Routines / Add-routine unchanged. Channel pane unchanged. Out of scope: marketplace catalog, iOS Open/Record/takeover, `/` autocomplete, blank New skill (create stays v0.16 teach-a-task). See §24.

**v0.19 (CEO 2026-08-26):** iOS takeover. **iOS only** (`ios/`). One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** Desktop Open/Done stays v0.15 — no `desktop/` chrome work. Runtime protocol **unchanged**: reuse v0.15 `POST/DELETE /v1/agents/{id}/computer/session` plus pointer/key. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0** (v0.15/v0.18 contract). `recording` stays unused. No iOS Record, pinch-zoom, marketplace, or a separate window. See §25.

**v0.20 (CEO 2026-08-27):** iOS Record. **iOS only** (`ios/`). One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** Desktop Record/Stop/Save stays v0.16 — no `desktop/` chrome work. Runtime protocol **unchanged**: reuse v0.16 `POST/DELETE /v1/agents/{id}/computer/record` then `POST /v1/agents/{id}/skills { name }`. Discard is omit that POST. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0** unless docs-only bump. Out: marketplace, pinch-zoom, blank New skill, desktop changes. See §26.

**v0.21 (CEO 2026-08-27):** Composer **`/`** skill autocomplete. **1:1 agent composer only.** `/` at the **start of a token** opens the existing **`@`** typeahead overlay (not a second system): **240px / 240pt**, `--bg-elevated`, **1px** border, **8px / 8pt** radius, rows **36px** desktop / **44pt** iOS, name **14px / 14pt**, **no avatar**, filter by **name**. ↑↓ + Enter or click/tap inserts **`/name` as plain text** (not a chip, not an `@`). Esc closes. **Send** loads that agent’s **SKILL.md** into the turn. Unknown **`/foo`** stays plain text. Channel **`/`** is plain text — **no popup, no typeahead, no load path**. Empty list or no match: **no popup, no empty copy**. **No extra chip chrome** in the transcript. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0** — typeahead uses existing **`GET /v1/agents/{id}/skills`**. No second PR. Out: marketplace, blank New skill, `@skill`. See §27.

**v0.22 (CEO 2026-08-27):** Blank New skill. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR. No new HTTP routes.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0** (optional `body` on existing `POST /skills`). Skills header trailing 12px / 12pt `Add`. Empty `No skills yet.` still shows Add. 320px sheet title **`New skill`**. Primary `Add` disabled until name AND body. `POST { name, body }` writes SKILL.md (no capture). `POST { name }` omitted body stays record-to-skill. Out: marketplace, `@skill`, Slack/GitHub builder. See §28.

**v0.23 (CEO 2026-08-27):** Slack/GitHub inbound listeners. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR. No new HTTP routes.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. POST `/v1/agents/{id}/routines` stays one route, cron XOR trigger. Slack/GitHub segments on the Add-routine sheet **only if** that MCP plugin is `connected` (omit, do not disable; one 12px row). Slack field placeholder `#eng`, hint `Channel the bot is in.`; GitHub `owner/name`, hint `One repo. No wildcards.` Fire is that agent’s 1:1 as A. Out: marketplace, VNC, event picker. See §29.

**v0.24 (CEO 2026-08-27):** Curated plugin catalog (not a store). One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. New route only: `GET /v1/plugins/catalog` (Bearer). Bare array `{ id, name, transport, command?, args?, url? }`. Exactly two entries when neither is installed (Slack then GitHub). Catalog Add is existing `POST /v1/plugins` (no extra sheet). Omit a row when that kind is already in `GET /v1/plugins` (connected or needsAuth). Empty catalog is 200 `[]` (hide Catalog header). Custom Add stays. Out: public store, search, VNC, extra channel types. See §30.

**v0.25 (CEO 2026-08-27):** Chat attachments. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. `POST /v1/agents/{id}/attachments` multipart field `file` → **201** `{ id, kind, name, url, size }` (`kind` is `image` or `file`). `url` is Bearer `GET /v1/attachments/{id}`. Over 10MB or `video/*` → **422**. Message POST adds `attachmentIds[]`; empty `content` is ok if that list is non-empty. Unknown/foreign ids → 422. GET message grows `attachments` (user-right only this slice). Runtime includes them in that turn. Existing `{ mime, data }` images stay off-model. No `/v1/chats/` resource. Composer + user-right only; agent-sent attachments and video wait. See §31.

**v0.26 (CEO 2026-08-27):** Agent-sent attachments. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No new client HTTP routes. GET `attachments` on any `kind=message` (user-right or agent LEFT); `kind=tool` / `widget` / `connect` stay `[]`. Runtime binds `write_file` and computer screenshot files onto that assistant `kind=message` (same store + Bearer GET). Composer and POST `/v1/agents/{id}/attachments` stay user-only. LEFT chrome matches user-right (images 220×160; files 36px name chips). Out: video, VNC, public store, extra channel types, agent drag-drop, public/unauthed URLs, a second upload route, agent-sent video. See §32.

**v0.27 (CEO 2026-08-27):** Video attachments. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No new HTTP routes. No `/v1/chats/` resource. Lift the v0.25 `video/*` 422. Same POST `/v1/agents/{id}/attachments` multipart `file` → **201** `{ id, kind, name, url, size }`. `kind` is `image` | `file` | `video`. Video max **50MB** (`Max 50MB.`); image/file stay **10MB** (`Max 10MB.`). Empty still 422. GET attachments on any `kind=message`. Runtime does **not** send video bytes to the model. Agent `write_file` of a video under 50MB may bind as `kind=video` on the assistant `kind=message`. Composer drop/pick video is a pending chip (not the v0.25 danger line). Bubble player **220×160**, native controls, no autoplay, user-right and agent LEFT. See §33.

**v0.28 (CEO 2026-08-27):** watch-video tool. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No new client HTTP. No `/v1/chats/` resource. Desktop + iOS idle (no chrome; player stays the v0.27 220×160). Built-in `watch_video` `{ attachmentId }` auto-runs like write_file / shell / web_fetch. Agent decides when to call it. Unknown / foreign / non-video → tool error (POST messages stays 200). Success is plain text into the tool result; chrome is the existing 12px muted `kind=tool` line `Watched {name}`. Not a second player, not on the user-right, not a widget. Do not auto-inject every video. Bytes still never go into the user turn. See §34.

**v0.29 (CEO 2026-08-27):** IME Enter + create agent/channel tools. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No new HTTP routes. No `/v1/chats/`. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Composer: Enter does not send while IME is composing (`isComposing` / `keyCode 229` / `markedTextRange`). Desktop + iOS. Send still works after composition commits. Shift+Enter newline unchanged. Send button unchanged. Built-in auto-run tools wrapping existing `POST /v1/agents`: `create_agent` `{ name, title?, description? }`; `create_channel` `{ name, memberIds? }` (`kind=channel` on that same POST). Tool line / `done_summary`: `Created {name}`. Empty name or unknown memberIds → tool `Error:` (user POST stays 200). Unknown memberIds on the HTTP create stay 422. Isolation: creating B does not paint B’s 1:1 into A’s chat. Seed SKILL.md (v0.9 load): 项目 → `create_channel`, 员工 → `create_agent`. No new Settings chrome. Out: extra channel types, VNC, store, deleting/renaming via chat, Mac folder picker, a confirm widget. See §35.

**v0.30 (CEO 2026-08-27):** Composer clipboard paste. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No new HTTP routes. No `/v1/chats/`. Runtime idle. Paperclip and drag-drop stay exactly as v0.25/v0.27. While the composer is focused (text field or composer bar), paste (Cmd-V / Ctrl-V and the clipboard `paste` event) of image/file/video becomes the same pending chips (56×56 image thumb + ×; 56×56 video poster + 16px play + ×; 36px file chip name+size + ×). Clipboard bitmap with no filename is `kind=image` named `image.png` (jpeg→`image.jpg`, gif→`image.gif`, webp→`image.webp`). Finder copy keeps real names. Text-only paste stays in the textarea (do not intercept; do not make a `.txt` chip). Mixed: files/images → chips; text still inserts. Same 10MB / 50MB danger copy as drop. Send on if text or any pending chip (paste-only image still sends). Do not change transcript chrome, LEFT attachments, or the player. iOS UIPasteboard paste matches the paperclip chips. Out: VNC, extra channel types, public store, voice, mermaid/math, agent-created routine confirm card, Mac folder picker, screenshot-from-Box, event-picker, marketplace, new Message kinds, intercepting text paste. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. See §36.

**v0.31 (CEO 2026-08-28):** Copy / Regenerate on assistant messages. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No new path. No `/v1/chats/`. `POST /v1/agents/{id}/messages` body `{ "regenerate": true }` (empty/omitted content). Copy on completed LEFT `kind=message` (1:1 and channel). Regenerate 1:1 only, latest completed LEFT `kind=message`, hidden while a stream is in flight. Click does not paint a new user-right bubble. Truncate that `kind=message` plus trailing `kind=tool` lines of that turn, then stream a replacement. 422/409 as locked. Composer / paperclip / clipboard paste / IME Enter / `/` typeahead unchanged. Out: thumbs, edit-user-message, composer mic, mermaid/math, voice, routine confirm, shell Approve, VNC, marketplace/store. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. See §37.

**v0.32 (CEO 2026-08-28):** shell Approve. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new path. No `/approve` route. Mutating `shell` pauses and emits a LEFT `kind=approve` card (dedicated kind — NOT a widget fork, NOT `kind=widget`). Body is `{ command }` plus `approveStatus: pending`. Persist, then `message.done` with the full Message, then the stream ends (same SSE shape as v0.8 widgets). No `approve.*` event. Answer is POST `{ approveReply: { id, approved: true } }` or `{ id, dismissed: true }` on the same transcript — not a user-right bubble. Dismissed is Deny (including the card ×). After Approve: run that command, then the existing 12px muted `kind=tool` line (`Ran {cmd}`). After Deny: patch the SAME card to Denied (12px muted `Denied`); do not run; do not wake. Read-only shells auto-run with no card: `ls`, `cat`, `pwd`, `git status`, `git log`, `git diff` (those exact commands, including simple flags/paths that stay read-only). Any chain, pipe, redirection, `&&` / `;` / `|`, or anything else MUST gate. Content send 409 while a card is pending (one pending approve per transcript). Same 409 if the model emits a second approve while one is pending. Other tools stay auto-run. Widgets / connect unchanged. Isolation matches widgets. Channel: approve only in a thread (`replyTo` set), never the timeline. Dedicated renderer on desktop and iOS. No Settings chrome. No new composer UI. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. See §38.

**v0.33 (CEO + Design 2026-08-30):** Agent-created routines + user-right bubble wrap. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No new HTTP routes. No `/v1/chats/`. Built-in tools wrap existing POST/PATCH/DELETE `/v1/agents/{id}/routines`: `create_routine`, `pause_routine`, `delete_routine`. Create and delete end the turn on a LEFT `kind=widget` confirm (v0.8 chrome, not `kind=approve`, no new card). Create prompt `Save "{name}" for {when}?` (cron `scheduleLabel`; webhook `as a webhook`; Slack `Slack #eng`; GitHub `GitHub owner/repo`; fallback `Save "{name}"?`). No helpText. Primary Save / default Don't. × = Don't (muted Dismissed). After Save: check, then 12px `Scheduled {name}`. Don't/×: no persist. Delete prompt `Remove "{name}"?`. Danger Remove / default Keep. × = Keep. After Remove: check, then 12px `Removed {name}`. pause_routine auto-runs `Paused {name}` / `Resumed {name}`. Seed `routines` SKILL.md (定时 / 提醒). User-right + LEFT markdown wrap unbroken URLs (`overflow-wrap: anywhere`); fenced-code scroll stays. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Leave #23/#24 untouched. See §39.

**v0.34 (Design/Product/Frontend 2026-08-30, supersedes the CEO launch default-open / header Hide copy):** Collapse the desktop Computer pane. **Desktop only.** One impl PR. No new HTTP. v0.64 kept each chat's unsent text in memory for the session, so a restart dropped it, and iPad's list selection could move `selectedAgentID` before the draft was saved. Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-O (⌘⇧O / ⌃⇧O on iOS) marks every chat read through the existing v1.01 Mark all as read control. It clears every unread accent dot, including archived chats and channel handoff dots, so the window title and badge count go to zero and the Unread filter shows **No unread chats**. The chord is a no-op when nothing is unread. It still works while the composer is focused and does not insert text. A key repeat does not clear again and does not insert. IME composition keeps the key. Find, the quick switcher, the shortcuts list, rename, the lightbox, a modal or overlay text field, and a focused widget, approve, or connect card keep the key. It still clears when no chat is open, because the sidebar control does. Cmd/Ctrl-K, Cmd/Ctrl-F, Cmd/Ctrl-L, Cmd/Ctrl-N, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-C, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-A, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-Backspace, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-P, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-M, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-U, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-R, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-D, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-E, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-G, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-F, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-H, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-J, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-Y, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-S, Cmd/Ctrl-/, Cmd/Ctrl-1 through Cmd/Ctrl-9, Alt+Up/Down, PageUp/PageDown, Cmd/Ctrl-Home/End, Esc, and Up-arrow recall stay. The shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet include the chord. Keep v0.47–v1.19 intact. v0.98 is a separate open PR and is not this cut. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Leave stale PRs #23/#24/#130 alone. See §120.

**v1.21 (CEO 2026-10-16):** Jump to the previous unread chat from the keyboard. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP. Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-K (⌘⇧K / ⌃⇧K on iOS) opens the previous unread chat before the currently open one in the sidebar's current visible order, the same order Alt+Up/Down and Cmd/Ctrl-1..9 use, with the Pinned group first, wrapping around. Opening that chat is the same as clicking its row, so its draft and reading place come back and the composer is focused. If no chat in that order is unread, nothing changes. The open chat is not selected again when it is the only unread row. Muted chats do not count because they do not paint the accent dot. Archived chats are included only while that list is showing. The chord still works while the composer is focused and does not insert text. A key repeat does not jump again and does not insert. IME composition keeps the key. Find, the quick switcher, the shortcuts overlay, rename, the lightbox, a modal or overlay text field, and a focused widget, approve, or connect card keep the key. Cmd/Ctrl-K without Shift still opens the quick switcher. Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-J still opens the next unread chat. The shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet include the chord. Keep v0.47–v1.20 intact. v0.98 is a separate open PR and is not this cut. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Leave stale PRs #23/#24/#130 alone. See §121.

**v1.28 (CEO 2026-10-17):** Queue a follow-up while a reply is generating. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP. While a client-started assistant reply is in flight, Enter or Send holds the composer text as a muted **Queued** row just above the composer. The row stays editable and a small × removes it. Several rows are not combined. When that reply settles — it completed, it was empty, it errored, or the user pressed Stop or Esc — the oldest row is sent through the existing send path. Later rows wait, one per completed reply. Stop or Esc aborts the current reply and the next queued row goes out immediately. The queue is per chat (agent and thread) for the session and survives switching chats. It is not kept across relaunch. If that queued send fails, the chat's queue is dropped and the text is restored to that chat's composer. Pending attachment chips are not queued. IME composition and Shift+Enter do not queue. Esc still Stops. No new shortcut, so the shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet stay as they are. Reduce Motion adds no animation. Keep v0.47–v1.27 intact. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Leave stale PRs #23/#24/#130 alone. See §128.

**v1.29 (CEO 2026-10-17):** Soft-wrap toggle on assistant code blocks. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP. The v0.11 fence header already shows a 12px muted language label from the fenced info string (`python`, `ts`) and shows nothing when the fence is unlabeled. This cut does not add a second label. It adds a 12px muted **Wrap** button beside Copy on each completed assistant LEFT `kind=message` code fence. Default is no wrap, with horizontal scroll. Wrap soft-wraps that block's long lines, including a long token, and the label reads **Unwrap**. Each block is independent. The choice lasts for the app session and is not saved across relaunch. A rendered mermaid diagram keeps horizontal scroll and does not show Wrap; a failed diagram falls back to the fence and does. Hidden while that message is still streaming. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. No new shortcut, so the shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet stay as they are. Copy still copies the fence body only. Keep v0.47–v1.28 intact. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Leave stale PRs #23/#24/#130 alone. See §129.

**v1.30 (CEO 2026-10-17):** Transcript reading offset. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP. v0.74 already remembers, for the session, whether a chat was within ~64px of the bottom or parked on an earlier row, and coming back aligns that row's top with the viewport. That is why a scroll partway through a message lands at the top of the message. This cut stores how far into that row the viewport started, as a fraction of the row height, and restores that fraction. A zero fraction still aligns the row top. Within ~64px of the bottom, the chat still opens at the latest and stays pinned to the stream. An anchor that is no longer loaded opens at the latest. A reply that arrived while the chat was away shows Jump to latest and the New messages divider and does not yank the parked place. In-chat find, jump to a quoted message, jump to one of your sent messages, and Cmd/Ctrl-Home/End still scroll to their target. Send, Regenerate, and Jump still snap to the bottom. The restore assigns the offset with no animation. The place is session memory only and is not written to disk. Keep v0.47–v1.29 intact. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Leave stale PRs #23/#24/#130 alone. See §130.

**v1.31 (CEO 2026-10-19):** Markdown tables in completed assistant replies. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP. A completed assistant LEFT `kind=message` that contains a GitHub-style pipe table renders that table. A table wider than the bubble scrolls horizontally inside its own container. Columns stay on one line, so they are not squashed, and the table does not spill out of the bubble or widen the transcript. A 12px muted **Copy** button at the top-right copies that table's markdown source (the header, delimiter, and rows as written), then reads **Copied** for **1.5s** and reverts. Accessible name `Copy table` (and `Copied` while confirming). Markdown source, not tab-separated text, so a paste back into the composer round-trips and matches the other copy controls, which copy source. The control is hidden while that message is still streaming, and a streaming LEFT bubble stays plain text. A fence that contains pipes stays a code fence. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. No new shortcut, so the shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet stay as they are. User-right text, tool lines, and cards stay as they are. Keep v0.47–v1.30 intact. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. GFM `~~strikethrough~~` renders struck through on desktop. An own-line thematic break (`---`, `***`, `___`, spaces allowed between the marks) renders as a thin muted rule on desktop and iOS. A setext underline stays a heading. A fence or a `$$` block that contains these characters stays a fence or math. While the reply is still streaming, the LEFT bubble stays as it renders today. Copy on the message, Copy on a code block, and Copy on a table still copy markdown source. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. A completed assistant LEFT `kind=message` reference such as `[^1]` or `[^note]` renders as a small superscript marker. The matching definition `[^1]: note text` is removed from the body flow and shown as a muted, numbered Footnotes list at the end of the message. Numbers follow the first reference, and a later reference to the same label reuses that number. On desktop, clicking or activating the marker scrolls to and focuses its note, and a back-link returns to the reference. Do not bump. No new HTTP. A completed assistant LEFT `kind=message` resolves a full reference `[text][ref]`, a collapsed reference `[ref][]`, and a shortcut `[ref]` against a link reference definition such as `[ref]: https://example.com "Optional title"` anywhere in that same message. The label match is case-insensitive and whitespace-normalized. The resolved link looks and behaves like an existing inline link: the same styling, the same v1.35 destination preview on desktop hover or focus and on iOS long-press Open/Copy Link, and the same http(s) safe-scheme rules. Definition lines are not shown. An unresolved reference stays the literal characters. `[^1]` stays a footnote, not a reference link. Task lists, tables, code spans, and fenced code are unchanged, and a reference inside code is not parsed. While the reply is still streaming, the LEFT bubble stays as it renders today. Copy on the message, Copy on a code block, and Copy on a table still copy markdown source. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. **v1.40 (CEO 2026-10-20):** CommonMark backslash escapes and HTML character references in completed assistant replies. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP. A completed assistant LEFT `kind=message` treats a backslash before any ASCII punctuation as that literal character, so `\*not bold\*` and `5 \> 3` show the characters and do not start emphasis, a quote, a list, or a link. Named character references from the common HTML5 set (`amp`, `lt`, `gt`, `quot`, `apos`, `nbsp`, `copy`, `reg`, `trade`, `hellip`, `mdash`, `ndash`, quotes, `times`, `divide`, `deg`, `plusmn`, `middot`, `bull`, currency, arrows, and check marks) and decimal or hex numeric references (`&#8212;`, `&#x1F600;`) decode to their characters. An unknown name such as `&notanentity;` stays literal. A numeric reference that is zero, a surrogate, a noncharacter, a disallowed control, or past U+10FFFF becomes U+FFFD. Inside a code span or a fenced code block, backslashes and entities stay exactly as written. `&lt;script&gt;` is the text `<script>`, never HTML. Inline `\(` `\)` and `$$` math are recognized before escapes, so those delimiters stay math. Footnotes, reference links, tables, task lists, autolinks, the link destination preview, and syntax highlighting stay as they are. While the reply is still streaming, the LEFT bubble stays plain text. Copy on the message, Copy on a code block, and Copy on a table still copy markdown source. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. No new shortcut, so the shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet stay as they are. User-right text, tool lines, and cards stay as they are. Keep v0.47–v1.39 intact. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. **v1.41 (CEO 2026-10-20):** CommonMark hard line breaks in completed assistant replies. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP. A completed assistant LEFT `kind=message` renders a line that ends in two or more spaces, or in a backslash, followed by a newline inside a paragraph as a visible line break. The trailing spaces and that backslash are not shown. Leading spaces on the next line are ignored. A plain single newline is a soft break and renders as a space, so the paragraph stays one wrapping block. That matches CommonMark and Grok Bot's chat: blank lines already split bubbles, and the real-line-break fix is this hard-break rule, not a break on every newline. At the end of a paragraph or other block there is no break. Trailing spaces disappear, and a backslash stays visible. Inside a code span, a fenced code block, an indented code block, inline `\(` `\)` math, a `$$` block, a pipe table, or raw HTML, the markers stay literal. Escapes, footnotes, reference links, strikethrough, task lists, autolinks, the link destination preview, and syntax highlighting stay as they are. While the reply is still streaming, the LEFT bubble stays plain text. Copy on the message, Copy on a code block, and Copy on a table still copy markdown source. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. No new shortcut, so the shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet stay as they are. Reference-style `![alt][ref]` reuses the v1.39 definitions and then renders the same way. The alt text is the accessibility label. The title, when present, is the tooltip. While the image loads, the bubble shows a muted placeholder. If it fails, the alt text (or the URL when the alt is empty) shows as muted link-like text instead of a broken icon. Clicking or tapping the image opens the existing v0.79 lightbox. An image that is the entire content of a link, `[![alt](img)](href)`, opens that link destination instead, using the existing http(s) link rules; on desktop an image anywhere inside a link does the same. Only http(s) URLs and existing local attachment paths (`/v1/attachments/{id}`, `/v1/images/{id}`) load. javascript:, data:, file:, and any other scheme stay the literal characters. Inside a code span, a fenced code block, an indented code block, inline `\(` `\)` math, a `$$` block, or a code span in a table cell, the marker stays literal. A normal table cell can still show an image. While a LEFT reply is still streaming, the bubble stays plain text. Copy on the message, Copy on a code block, and Copy on a table still copy markdown source. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. A `mailto:` link opens only when the path is exactly an email address, with no query and no fragment. `<javascript:...>`, `<file:...>`, `<ftp:...>`, `<data:...>`, and a URI whose scheme is `mailto:` stay the literal characters, brackets included. `<not a link>`, a space inside the brackets, and a backslash before the opening bracket stay literal too. No new HTTP. A completed assistant LEFT `kind=message` renders a CommonMark setext heading the same way as an ATX heading. A line of text followed by a line of `=` is an H1. A line of `-` (at least two) is an H2. Desktop uses the existing 16px / 14px semibold `h1` / `h2`. iOS uses the same 16pt / 14pt semibold font. Several lines with no blank line between them, followed by the underline, are one heading, and a break between those lines stays inside it. A `---` after a blank line stays a thematic break. A `---` that is not indented into a list item stays a break after the list. The same underline indented to the item's text is a heading inside the item. A setext underline that would only match as a lazy continuation of a quote or list stays the literal characters. A backslash-escaped `\=` or `\-` stays literal. Underlines inside a fenced code block, an indented code block, inline `\(` `\)` math, a `$$` block, raw HTML, or a table cell stay literal. While a LEFT reply is still streaming, the bubble stays plain text. Copy on the message, Copy on a code block, and Copy on a table still copy markdown source. The domain must contain a dot. `+` is allowed before `@` and not after. A trailing `.` stays outside the link. A body row with more cells drops the extra ones, so `| abc | def |` / `| --- | --- |` / `| bar |` / `| bar | baz | boo |` renders two columns: `bar` and an empty cell, then `bar` and `baz`. `boo` is not a column. A shorter row pads with empty cells, and those cells keep that column's alignment. The table does not grow a left-aligned column. Copy on the table still copies the original lines, including `boo`. A line with no pipe still ends the table. A blank line, a list, a quote, a fence, and four-space indent still end it too. While a LEFT reply is still streaming, the bubble stays plain text. Copy on the message still copies markdown source. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. No new shortcut, so the shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet do not gain a row. Keep v0.47–v1.64 intact. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Leave stale PRs #23/#24/#130 alone. See §165.

**v1.66 (CEO 2026-10-22):** GFM pipe-less table rows continue the table in completed assistant replies. One impl PR covering desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP. Pipe tables already shipped in v1.31. Column alignment already shipped in v1.55. Clipping a wide row and padding a short row already shipped in v1.65. v1.65 left the pipe-less continuation out of scope. This cut is that row. A completed assistant LEFT `kind=message` keeps a body line with no unescaped pipe as one cell, then pads it to the header, so `| abc | def |` / `| --- | --- |` / `| bar | baz |` / `bar` / blank / `bar` is two rows (`bar`/`baz`, then `bar` and an empty cell) and the paragraph `bar` stays outside. A heading, thematic break, quote, list (including an empty `-` or `1.`), fence, four-column indent, raw HTML block (including a type 7 tag on its own line), or `$$` math line still ends the table. `#foo`, `===`, `*foo`, and a ten-digit `1234567890. item` stay cells. A pipe-less header does not start a table, so `foo` over `---` stays a setext heading. A `[ref]:` or `[^1]:` line inside the table stays in the cell. The same line after a blank line is still a definition. While a LEFT reply is still streaming, the bubble stays plain text. Copy on the message and Copy on the table still copy markdown source, including the pipe-less line. No animation, including when Reduce Motion is off. No new shortcut, so the shortcuts list and the iOS Gestures & shortcuts sheet do not gain a row. Keep v0.47–v1.65 intact. Out: VNC, extra channels, store, coding-agent, new surfaces. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Leave stale PRs #23/#24/#130 alone. See §166.

v0 still stands: local inference on DGX Spark, macOS desktop + thin iOS companion, no Cursor login. This spec adds two features on top of the chat loop.

## Problem

1. Chat reads as a log. Grok Bot (and iMessage) put the user on the right and everyone else on the left. Desktop and iOS must match.
2. Chinghau created a second agent and expected Grok-Bot-like teammate messaging. Isolated 1:1 transcripts mean agents cannot see or reach each other.

## Not in v0.1

No MCP, connectors, skills, sandbox/box GUI, computer-use screenshot pane,
routines, or question widgets. No copying a private 1:1 into a channel unless
the user asked to tell the room (an `@` mention is that ask). One seeded
channel plus extra user-created channels (v0.4). Basic file/shell/web tools
are **v0.5** (runtime-owned; not in the client). The thin desktop Computer
pane (file tree + text preview over that sandbox) is **v0.6**. Runtime MCP
(stdio + LAN, `mcp.json` on disk) is **v0.7**. Full sandbox
computer GUI (browser, VNC, terminal) stays later.

## 1. Chat layout (desktop + iOS)

- User messages: RIGHT. Distinct user fill. No avatar required on the right.
- Agent messages: LEFT, with that agent's avatar + display name on the first bubble of a streak.
- Consecutive bubbles from the same speaker stack. A speaker change starts a new streak.
- Streaming tokens render in-place in the left bubble.
- Do not show raw runtime cues (`[agent]`, `[Group chat:]`, `[routine]`) to the user.
- Alignment is a client rule: `author.kind == user` → right; anything else → left.
- A 1:1 never shows another agent's bubble, an involve line, or muted `to B` / `from A` chrome.

## 2. Surfaces

Two surfaces, both required.

### Group channel (shared transcript)

- Seed one channel named `Snorlax-Bot` (id `snorlax-bot-group`, kind `channel`). Every agent is a member. New agents auto-join it.
- The seed 1:1 is a **distinct** resource: id `snorlax-bot`, name **Snorlax**, title Assistant, kind `agent`. Never reuse `snorlax-bot` as the channel transcript.
- Sidebar lists both as normal rows. The channel row shows a muted “Channel” subtitle from `kind` (12px / 12pt), not by guessing id. Never two rows named “Snorlax-Bot”. Do not show ids in chrome.
- One shared channel transcript. User can read and send.
- User messages: right. Agent posts: left, identified.
- Posts are async. Nobody waits for a reply. Unmentioned members stay silent unless they have something material to add.
- **This is the display surface for agent–agent traffic.** If A talks to B, it appears here, not in A's 1:1 or B's 1:1.

### Agent 1:1 (user ↔ that agent only)

- A 1:1 with agent A (including Snorlax at `snorlax-bot`) shows **only** the user (right) and A (left).
- No other agent's bubbles. No involve line `{User} mentioned you in {A}`. No muted `to B` / `from A` DM chrome.
- GET/list and SSE must not paint peer messages into a 1:1. Filter: `senderId` is `user` or that agent. The channel is shared and is not filtered that way.

## 3. `@` syntax

- Mentions resolve by agent id from the typeahead chip. The composer sends `mentions: [id, ...]`.
- Unresolved `@text` (typed but not picked from typeahead) is NOT a mention. It stays plain text. Do not 422 the send just because someone typed `@foo`.
- **422 only for an unknown chip id** (and `@everyone` chip in a 1:1). Unknown/ambiguous `@text` in the body is omitted.
- No display-name prefix matching on user sends. Exact `@DisplayName` in the body may still route (same as a resolved chip).
- Multiple mentions are allowed on one send, bounded by the hop / peer-send caps in section 5. A one-mention-per-send cap is rejected.
- `@everyone` is valid only in a group channel (all members are addressed). Invalid as a chip in 1:1. Typed `@everyone` in a 1:1 is plain text.
- **Group channel:** the message is stored in that channel only. Mentioned agents are addressed and should reply. Unmentioned agents stay silent by default.
- **1:1 with agent A:** `@B …` stays in A's transcript as the user's bubble (right). A replies in that 1:1. B does **not** appear in A's 1:1. B does **not** get an involve bubble in B's 1:1. B is addressed in a **handoff thread** on the seeded channel (v0.2).
- Agent-authored `@Name` notifies that member the same way a user mention does. If that mention originates in a 1:1, the peer traffic still lands in the channel.
- Mentions are highlighted in the bubble. Composer offers `@` autocomplete of agent display names (desktop + iOS). Inserting a chip keeps a stable caret after the chip; the chip stays a chip.

## 4. What appears in which transcript

- User talks to A in 1:1 → only A's 1:1. User right, A left.
- User `@B` in A's 1:1 → A's 1:1 keeps the user-right chip (plus a jump line when a handoff was created) and A's reply. B does not appear in A's or B's 1:1. The seeded channel gets a LEFT `kind=handoff` root authored as A (`from {A}` + `userAsk`, brief collapsed as Context) and a flat thread of replies. Not a user-right copy in the channel.
- A talks to B (agent-authored `@B` or a routed mention) → the same channel thread, not A's 1:1 or B's 1:1. No jump chip unless the user caused the handoff.
- Anyone posts in the group channel → that channel transcript. Mentioned agents wake. 1:1s are unchanged unless the user is also chatting there separately.

Privacy: a 1:1 with the user is private. Do not relay it into a channel or to another agent unless the user `@` mentioned them or explicitly asked to tell them.

## 5. Loop / hop limits

Locked. Do not implement hop 0→1-only.

- User → current agent is hop 0.
- Max hop depth: 3 (A→B→C→D ok; D→E dropped).
- Max peer sends (DMs + channel posts) per originating user turn: 4.
- Same-edge cap: A→B→A once per turn; a second A→B in that turn is dropped.
- Dropped messages are silent in the UI. Runtime logs them. Sender is not prompted to retry.
- Delivery is always async. Recipients never block the sender.

These caps are unchanged in v0.1.1. Only the **display surface** for peer traffic changed: channel, not 1:1s.

## 6. Acceptance checks

Desktop (macOS) and iOS must each pass:

- User bubble is right; the current agent's bubble is left with name + avatar.
- In the seeded channel, a second agent's posts are left and labeled as them, never as the user.
- From agent A's 1:1, user sends `@B hello`. A's 1:1 shows the user (right), a jump line “Also in Snorlax-Bot” when `handoff` is set, and A (left) only. B's 1:1 is empty of that turn. The channel timeline shows a compact handoff row; the thread has A's card and B's reply.
- Scrolling a long 1:1 or channel transcript does not scroll the 256px agent list. Only the transcript pane scrolls.
- Right-click (desktop) or swipe/context (iOS) deletes a roster row after confirm `Delete {name}? This removes the agent and its chat.` (channel copy: `the channel and its chat`). That includes seed `snorlax-bot` and seed channel `snorlax-bot-group` (DELETE 204, no auto-reseed, never recreate). Delete is on the sidebar row, not in the info pane. After seed channel delete, if the roster is nonempty, select an **agent** first, else a remaining channel. Empty roster keeps chrome (sidebar, header, composer). No jump chip unless the target channel is still in the roster.
- `@` autocomplete lists agent names. Picking a chip keeps the caret after the chip. Unresolved `@text` stays plain text; send does not error.
- Streaming tokens stay left-aligned.
- Fourth hop is dropped (debug/test hook is enough).
- Two mentions on one user send both deliver (inside the peer-send cap).

iOS-only: agent list includes the seeded channel; user can read and send in 1:1 and in that channel over LAN.

## 7. Locked decisions (2026-08-25)

1. User `@Name` in a 1:1 is runtime-routed to that agent (not "model must remember to send").
2. One seeded `Snorlax-Bot` channel; all agents auto-join it **while that row exists**. Extra user-created channels are v0.4 (`POST /v1/agents` with `kind=channel`). If the seed channel is gone, new agents join nothing.
3. Hop limit 3, max 4 peer sends per user turn, same-edge once. Hop 0→1 / one-mention-per-send is rejected.
4. **v0.1.1:** Agent–agent traffic displays in a channel, not in the two 1:1 transcripts. 1:1 isolation overrides the earlier "DM-in-1:1s / involve bubble" rule. Seed `snorlax-bot` is the Snorlax 1:1 only. v0.4 1:1 @involves log on seed `snorlax-bot-group` when present (ignore body `channelId`); if gone, log on body `channelId` if it is an existing channel, else skip the log.
5. 422 only for an unknown typeahead chip id (and `@everyone` chip outside the group).
6. Roster names: seed 1:1 is **Snorlax**; channel is **Snorlax-Bot**. Clients show muted “Channel” from `kind`. Never two rows named Snorlax-Bot. Do not show ids in chrome.
7. Collaboration fluency is **v0.2** (handoff pack / channel thread / jump chip). v0.1.1 isolation stays.
8. **v0.3:** Header info pane. kind=agent is editable identity; kind=channel is a read-only member list. Seed agent DELETE is 204 and is not auto-reseeded. User-created channel DELETE is 204 (v0.4). Seed channel DELETE is 204 (CEO 2026-08-26).
9. **v0.4:** Report-back into A's 1:1 (not a hop; report as each peer lands). Extra channels with member editor. Isolation unchanged. CEO 2026-08-26: seed channel DELETE 204, no auto-reseed; A2A log is seed, else body `channelId` if it is a real channel, else skip.
10. **v0.5:** Runtime-owned tools in a workspace jail. Clients never call the model or the tools. No computer pane this slice. Four CEO locks: (1) channel project is a sandbox dir under `~/.snorlax-bot`, not a Mac folder picker; (2) shell has no extra network — HTTP is `web_search` / `web_fetch` only; (3) tools auto-run (no approval/question widgets); (4) search provider is env/config (`SNORLAX_SEARCH_PROVIDER` / `SNORLAX_SEARCH_URL`), not hardcoded.
11. **v0.6:** Thin Computer pane over the existing sandbox. Desktop 320px right column (collapsible, default open). iOS: no pane. Same roots as `workspace_for()`. Clients never call tools. No `projectPath` / folder picker. Isolation unchanged. Seed channel DELETE 204, no reseed. Seed PATCH identity 409 except `sharedProject`.
12. **v0.7:** FastAPI runtime is the MCP client. Desktop/iOS never speak MCP. `mcp.json` under `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR`. stdio + LAN HTTP/SSE. Namespaced `server__tool`; built-ins win. No marketplace UI this slice.

Runtime does not rewrite assistant text to HTML or split one message into many. SSE `message.delta` is still text chunks of that same string. User messages stay plain text as stored. Keep widget / connect / tool / routine fields. No MCP mix-in. Drop the grey agent bubble on LEFT `kind=message`. User-right stays plain. No mermaid, math, or raw HTML.

GET and `POST .../auth` stay v0.10. `kind=connect` isolation unchanged. Runtime owns MCP. Clients never speak MCP. No store / search catalog. Keep v0.8 widgets, v0.9 routines, seed DELETE 204, content string. Do not reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
