OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No `/v1/chats/` resource. Out: agent-sent attachments, video, public/unauthed URLs, a second upload route, VNC. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
28. **v0.26:** Agent-sent attachments. Runtime + desktop + iOS. No new client HTTP routes. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. GET `attachments: [{ id, kind, name, url, size }]` on any `kind=message` (user-right or agent LEFT). `kind=tool`, `widget`, and `connect` stay `[]`. Composer and `POST /v1/agents/{id}/attachments` plus `MessageCreate.attachmentIds` stay user-only. Do not add an agent upload route. After a turn, if the agent used `write_file` or a computer screenshot, the runtime copies those files onto that assistant `kind=message` (same attachment table + Bearer `GET /v1/attachments/{id}`). Screenshot → `kind=image`. `write_file` → `kind=file`, or `kind=image` if the written path is `image/*`. Skip `video/*` and over 10MB. Bind onto the `kind=message`, never the tool-trace row. LEFT chrome matches user-right: images **220×160**; files 36px name chips (8px radius, 1px border, 13px) **above** the markdown, left-aligned, **6px** gap (iOS **6pt**; 36pt chips, 44pt hit); chip opens the Bearer URL. Empty `attachments[]`: no extra chrome. Do **not** paint on tool lines, widgets, Connect cards, or the channel timeline handoff card. Isolation: attachments ride the message (B’s assistant row is not in A’s 1:1). Channel thread: speaking agent’s message only. Out: video, VNC, public store, extra channel types, agent drag-drop, public/unauthed URLs, a second upload route, agent-sent video. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
29. **v0.27:** Video attachments. Runtime + desktop + iOS. No new HTTP routes. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Lift v0.25 `video/*` 422 (`Video isn’t supported yet.`). Same POST `/v1/agents/{id}/attachments` multipart `file` → **201** `{ id, kind, name, url, size }`. `Attachment.kind` is `image` | `file` | `video`. `image/*` (not video) → image, max 10MB (`Max 10MB.`). `video/*` or `.mp4`/`.mov`/`.m4v`/`.webm`/`.avi`/`.mkv` → video, max 50MB (`Max 50MB.`). else → file, max 10MB. Empty still 422. GET `/v1/attachments/{id}` Bearer unchanged. `MessageCreate.attachmentIds` may include video ids; empty content ok if the list is non-empty. GET attachments on any `kind=message`. `kind=tool` / `widget` / `connect` stay `[]`. Runtime MUST NOT send video bytes to the model (no transcription, no in-model watch; `apply_to_user_content` skips `kind=video`). Agent `write_file` of a video under 50MB may bind as `kind=video` on the assistant `kind=message`. Composer: video drop/pick is a pending chip (not the v0.25 danger line); over 50MB is 12px `--danger` `Max 50MB.`, send off. Bubble player **220×160**, native controls, no autoplay, poster at that size; user-right and agent LEFT on `kind=message` only (same placement as images). Chip/player opens Bearer GET. Isolation unchanged. Out: VNC, public store, extra channel types, transcription / watch-video tool, public/unauthed URLs, a second upload route. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
30. **v0.28:** watch-video tool. Runtime. Desktop + iOS idle (no chrome). No new client HTTP. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No `/v1/chats/`. Built-in `watch_video` `{ attachmentId }` (required) on the existing auto-run tools list. Resolve on this conversation only (1:1 agent id or channel id). Unknown or foreign id → tool result `Error: unknown attachment id` (do not 422 the user POST). `kind != video` → `Error: not a video`. Success is plain text (filename + short description, cap ~8k), never JSON, never raw/base64 video bytes. `done_summary`: `Watched {name}` / `watch_video failed`. Chrome is that existing 12px muted `kind=tool` line under the agent’s LEFT streak. Not a second player, not on the user-right, not a widget, no Watch button. Player stays v0.27 **220×160**. Do not auto-call / auto-inject every video. `apply_to_user_content` still skips video bytes (short `user attached {name}` stub; may include `attachmentId`). Description is local: ffprobe duration/size + a few ffmpeg frames captioned by the same local OpenAI-compat backend when vision is available; if frames/caption fail, name + duration/size is still success. Out: VNC, public store, extra channel types, live camera, client transcode UI, a new chrome surface, a new HTTP resource. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
31. **v0.29:** IME Enter + create agent/channel tools. Runtime + desktop + iOS. No new HTTP routes. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No `/v1/chats/`. Composer Enter does not send while IME is composing: desktop `isComposing` or `keyCode === 229`; iOS `markedTextRange` set. After composition commits, Enter sends as today. Shift+Enter newline unchanged. Send button unchanged. Built-in auto-run `create_agent` `{ name, title?, description? }` and `create_channel` `{ name, memberIds? }` wrap existing `POST /v1/agents` (`kind=channel` for the latter; empty `memberIds` still snapshots the roster). Do not invent a second create API. Tool line / `done_summary`: `Created {name}` (failure: `create_agent failed` / `create_channel failed`). Same 12px muted LEFT `kind=tool` family as `Watched {name}` / `Read`. Not a card, not a widget. Empty name → tool Error, not 422 on the user send. Unknown memberIds → tool Error (HTTP POST still 422). Isolation: creating B does not paint B’s 1:1 into A’s chat. Smallest roster refresh on `tool.done` so the new row appears. Seed SKILL.md (v0.9 load): 项目 → `create_channel`, 员工 → `create_agent`. No new Settings chrome. Out: extra channel types, VNC, store, deleting/renaming via chat, Mac folder picker, a confirm widget. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
32. **v0.30:** Composer clipboard paste. Desktop + iOS. Runtime idle. No new HTTP routes. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No `/v1/chats/`. Reuse `POST /v1/agents/{id}/attachments` + `attachmentIds[]`. Paperclip and drag-drop stay v0.25/v0.27. While the composer is focused (text field or composer bar), Cmd-V / Ctrl-V and the `paste` event of image/file/video become the same pending chips (56×56 image; 56×56 video + 16px play; 36px file). Bitmap with no filename is `image.png` (jpeg→`image.jpg`, gif→`image.gif`, webp→`image.webp`). Finder names stay. Text-only paste stays in the textarea — do not intercept; do not make a `.txt` chip. Mixed: files/images → chips; text still inserts. Same `Max 10MB.` / `Max 50MB.` as drop; in-limit video is allowed. Send on if text or any chip. iOS UIPasteboard paste matches paperclip chips. Do not change transcript chrome, LEFT attachments, or the player. Out: VNC, extra channel types, public store, intercepting text paste. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
33. **v0.31:** Copy / Regenerate on assistant messages. Desktop + iOS + runtime. No new path. OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. No `/v1/chats/`. Copy on completed LEFT `kind=message` (1:1 and channel): after attachments + markdown, 6px gap, 12px muted action row, 12px / 12pt `Copy`. Click copies `message.content` (markdown source); label `Copied` for 1.5s. Not on tool / widget / connect / user-right. Fenced-code Copy stays. Regenerate 1:1 only, latest completed LEFT `kind=message`, hidden while SSE / Thinking is in flight. POST `{ "regenerate": true }`. Truncate last assistant turn (`kind=message` + trailing `kind=tool`) and replay last user turn (content + attachmentIds + mentions) without a new user-right bubble. 422 no completed user→assistant turn or combined with non-empty content / attachmentIds / widgetReply / connectReply. 409 stream already open or channel. Out: thumbs, edit-user-message, voice, mermaid/math, VNC, store. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. See §37.

## 8. v0.2 — collaboration handoff (locked)

When a handoff happens (user `@chip` in A's 1:1, or A DMs B):

- A still answers in the 1:1 (user + A). Looping B does not suppress that.
- Pack B wakes with (not a quote): `{ originating: { id, name, title }, userAsk, brief, mentionedIds }`.
- `brief` = last 8 messages of that 1:1, user+A only, cap 2k chars, drop oldest.
- Agent DM: `userAsk` = A's text; `brief` = last 8 of A's 1:1.

### Channel thread

On `snorlax-bot-group`. Root is a LEFT handoff card authored as A (muted “from {A}” + `user_ask`; brief collapsed as Context). Flat thread under that root; later hops stay here. B/A post LEFT; user in the thread is RIGHT. Unread on the channel row.

GET channel messages must **not** dump thread replies onto the timeline. Timeline = handoff roots (and other unreplied-to roots). `?threadId=` (or `replyTo`) returns that thread.

### Jump chip (only when the USER caused the handoff)

Muted 12px / 12pt system line under the user-right bubble that has the `@chip`. Copy “Also in {channel name}” (accent on the name; seed is Snorlax-Bot). Not a bubble, no avatar, no B text. Whole line tappable → opens the channel at that thread. No `handoff` field → no chip. Second `@B` in that 1:1: reuse the same thread, new jump line under that bubble. Hop/cap silent drop → no `handoff` field, no chip.

Where: A's 1:1 = user, A, jump chip. B's 1:1 unchanged. Peer lives in the thread.

### API (additive)

Keep `senderId` / `hop` / `mentions`. Add `replyTo` on channel msgs. Originating user message gets `handoff: { channelId, threadId }` (no fake speaker). `kind: handoff` on the channel root. 422 still unknown chip id.

### Chrome

Channel timeline: one compact LEFT row per handoff: A's 20px avatar + name, muted “from {A}”, one-line `user_ask`, muted “N replies”. Tap opens the thread. Thread: back to timeline; root LEFT card (A's avatar + name, muted from A, `user_ask`, collapsed Context). Replies under it, same L/R. Unread = 6px accent dot on the channel roster row. Desktop first, iOS match.

Not in v0.2: widgets, box, tools, MCP, skills, extra channels. Do not reopen 1:1 isolation.

## 9. v0.3 — agent identity + info pane (locked)

Header click (whole header row: 24px / 24pt avatar + name) opens an info pane. Desktop also **Cmd+Shift+I**. Close is **X** on the pane. Opening the pane does not change the 1:1 transcript. Desktop: **320px overlay on the chat**, not a third column. iOS: sheet.

**Agent vs channel are different panes.** Never treat `snorlax-bot-group` as an agent profile.

### Agent pane (`kind=agent`, including seed Snorlax)

72px / 72pt avatar (initials from name if empty), 16px / 16pt name, 13px / 13pt muted title + description. Gear beside X edits those four. Save is `PATCH { name, title, description, avatar }` to `/v1/agents/{id}`. Id is not editable. Empty avatar → initials. Avatar is a PATCH string or null (data URI or persisted `/v1/images/{id}`); no second upload API.

CEO override: seed `snorlax-bot` DELETE is **204**, gone from GET, no auto-reseed. Empty roster is fine. CEO override 2026-08-26: channel `snorlax-bot-group` DELETE is **204**, gone from GET, no auto-reseed — never recreate that id. Empty roster is OK (no agents and/or no channels) and **keeps chrome**. Delete is sidebar right-click / iOS swipe on the **row**, **not** in the info pane. Seed channel row gets the same Delete as other rows. After seed agent delete, if the roster is only the channel, select **Snorlax-Bot** — do not fake a seed. After seed channel delete, if anything remains, select an **agent** first, else a remaining channel. No jump chip if no channel remains. Seed identity PATCH is allowed. Seed channel identity PATCH stays 409. User-created: PATCH + DELETE as today.

### Channel pane (`kind=channel`)

**Seed `snorlax-bot-group`:** Read-only identity. Display name + muted Channel + member list from `memberIds` (avatar + name + title each). No gear, no PATCH name, no avatar editor. 44px / 44pt member rows, not tappable in this slice. All agents auto-join; DELETE 204 (CEO 2026-08-26), no auto-reseed, never recreate. Delete is sidebar/swipe on the row, not in this pane.

**User-created channel:** Same member rows. Gear beside X edits `name` + `memberIds` (`PATCH { name, memberIds }`). Seed pane stays read-only.

Header keeps showing live name/title/avatar after PATCH. Roster updates too. 1:1 is still user+A only.

Not in v0.3: computer preview (no placeholder), routines list, connector/Channels row, per-agent notifications, shape/color beyond avatar, create-agent wizard, sidebar sections.

## 10. v0.4 — report-back + extra channels (locked)

Keep isolation: GET/transcript of A's 1:1 is still only user + A. B does not appear as B. No involve bubble, no “from Test” speaker in the 1:1. Jump chip “Also in {channel}” may remain as an optional inspect link under the user's @ message only; it is **not** required to get the answer.

### Report-back

1. User `@chip` B in A's 1:1 (typeahead id). Runtime wakes B with the existing handoff pack (`originating`, `userAsk`, brief last 8, `mentionedIds`) in a **seed** `snorlax-bot-group` thread when that channel exists (body `channelId` ignored). If the seed is gone and the 1:1 POST body has `channelId` that is an existing `kind=channel` row, log there. Else skip the log (no `handoff` field, no jump chip, no fake seed). Unknown/stale `channelId`: skip, do not 422, do not recreate seed. B still wakes (not written to B's 1:1); A still reports in A's 1:1 as A (non-hop, `senderId=A`, no `from` field). Extra channels are a separate create flow; 1:1 involves never open a new channel per mention.
2. B's system/wake: you were asked to **do** the task. First reply is the work product. Do not acknowledge that you can. Do not ping-pong. Prompt/task only — **no ACK classifier** in code (do not detect “I can help”). FYI with no ask → stay silent (`looks_like_ask` is FYI silence, not an ACK retry).
3. When B's channel-thread turn completes (`message.done`) **or B is hop/cap dropped**, enqueue a generation for A on A's 1:1. This is **not** a peer send. It does **not** increment hop or spend hop budget. Hop caps only apply to A2A/channel peer sends.
4. Prompt to A (not a Message field): `{ result, from: { id, name, title }, threadId, userAsk }`. Hop-drop: `result` is a miss so A still tells the user B wasn't reached.
5. Persist a normal assistant Message in A's 1:1: `role=assistant`, `senderId=A`, `senderName`/`avatar`=A, **no mentions of B**, **no `from` field on the Message**. Isolation: never write B into A's 1:1. B's 1:1 stays empty.
6. Multiple `@chips`: fire A's report as **each** B lands (don't wait-for-all). Serialize A's report-back gens on that 1:1 so tokens don't interleave (insert the report-back as the next job after that peer).
7. Hold the originating 1:1 POST SSE open until every mentioned B has done or been dropped **and** A has reported for each. Extra `message.delta` / `message.done` on the **same** stream. No new SSE event type.
8. Chrome: report-back is another of A's **LEFT** bubbles in the 1:1 (20px avatar+name, no from B). Jump chip only under the user's @ message. Hop-drop is still A's LEFT bubble, no system chrome. Do not duplicate `from {Name}:` inside bubble body.
9. Channel thread remains the A2A log. Timeline = handoff roots; `?threadId=` = thread. `@mention` inside an extra channel stays in **that** channel's thread.

### Extra channels

- Seeded `snorlax-bot-group` / Snorlax-Bot stays on first empty DB. Auto-joins all agents. PATCH 409. DELETE 204, no auto-reseed, never recreate (CEO 2026-08-26). No member editor on seed this slice. After that delete, clients select an agent first if any remain, else a remaining channel.
- User can create additional channels: `POST /v1/agents` `{ name, kind: "channel", memberIds }` → 201. Omit kind → still create agent. `memberIds` are agent ids only; 422 unknown id / channel id in the list / missing name. Omitted memberIds snapshot every current agent. New agents auto-join `snorlax-bot-group` only if that row still exists; if the seed channel is gone they join nothing. Not extras.
- User-created channel PATCH `{ name, memberIds }` → 200 (add/remove members). DELETE 204. Agent DELETE (including seed `snorlax-bot`) stays 204 from v0.3. No new upload route.
- Desktop + iOS: `+` opens a **160px / 160pt** menu: New agent / New channel. New channel is a **320px overlay / iOS sheet**: name + member picker (44px rows, 28px avatar, checkbox, agents only). User-created channel pane has a gear; seed pane stays read-only. Delete is sidebar/swipe on every row including the seed channel; not in the info pane. Empty roster keeps chrome. No jump chip if the target channel is gone.

Not in v0.4: widgets, box, MCP, skills, vision. Tools land in v0.5.

## 11. v0.5 — basic tools (locked)

Match Grok Bot’s working tools (files, shell, web), not the full computer GUI
/ live preview. Clients never call the model or the tools. The runtime runs
OpenAI-compat function calling against oMLX/vLLM (mock in CI) and continues
the completion. Cap **8** tool rounds.

CEO locks (2026-08-26), do not reopen this slice:

1. **Channel project is a sandbox dir** under `~/.snorlax-bot` (or
   `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR`). It is **not** a picker for a folder on the host Mac.
   Extra fields such as `projectPath` are ignored.
2. **Shell has no extra network.** HTTP is `web_search` / `web_fetch` only.
   Do not curl/wget from shell.
3. **Tools auto-run.** The runtime executes tool calls immediately. No
   approval widget, no question widget, no “allow this command?” this slice.
4. **Search provider is configuration** (`SNORLAX_SEARCH_PROVIDER`, optional
   `SNORLAX_SEARCH_URL` with `{query}`), not a hardcoded vendor in the loop.

### Workspaces

- Agent workspace: `~/.snorlax-bot/workspaces/agents/{agentId}/` (or under
  `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR`). Created on first tool use. Isolated from the host home
  directory.
- Channel project: **always** `~/.snorlax-bot/workspaces/channels/{channelId}/`
  (or under `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR`). Members share that sandbox. Created lazily
  on first tool use in that channel (seed + user-created). Never a host-folder
  picker.
- 1:1 turns: tools run in that agent’s workspace.
- Channel / handoff thread turns: tools run in the channel sandbox (created
  on first tool use).
- Path jail: file tools cannot escape the active workspace root. Shell cwd
  is that root; `HOME` is the workspace, not the host home. Do not pass
  Docker/SSH host secrets into the tool env.

### Built-in tools

`list_dir`, `read_file`, `write_file`, `delete_file`, `shell` (no extra
network), `web_search` (configured provider; default DuckDuckGo HTML, no
paid key), `web_fetch` (GET, text/markdown, size cap). HTTP is the two web
tools only.

### Prompting

Agents know they have these tools. Tools run immediately — do not ask the
user to approve. Do not ACK ping-pong. Write code to files; do not dump a
whole app in the chat bubble. If B needs A’s files, they must be in a
channel sandbox or A must put them there. 1:1 isolation means B cannot see
A’s private workspace.

### API / UI

Additive. `/v1` chat contract stays. SSE `tool.start` / `tool.done` with
`{ id, name, summary, ok?, senderId, senderName }`. Tool activity also
**persists** as Message `kind=tool` with short content (“Read src/a.ts”,
“Ran ls”, “Fetched …”), same `senderId` as the speaking agent, so GET
matches live SSE. Not JSON, not a user-right bubble. Rows live only in the
transcript where that agent is speaking (1:1 if A tools; channel thread if
B tools). A’s 1:1 report-back is a normal assistant `kind=message` as A and
**must not** copy B’s tool lines. After any tool round the turn always
finishes with a normal assistant `kind=message` (streamed and persisted),
even if tools failed, the model returned empty text, or inference errored
after tools already ran — do not end on only a `kind=tool` line or
`event:error` with no saved assistant bubble. The final answer is a normal
LEFT bubble.
Unknown events must be ignored. No computer preview, no file-browser API,
no folder/path field (`sharedProject` is the boolean toggle). iOS sees chat
and those status lines only — **no file browser** this slice.

Not in v0.5: MCP, browser-use GUI, computer screenshot pane, widgets, host
folder picker, extra shell network, iOS file browser. Seeded channel DELETE
is 204, no auto-reseed (main / #32). Seed identity PATCH stays 409.

## 12. v0.6 — computer pane (locked)

Thin file browser over the **existing** tool sandbox. Not full B4 (no real
browser, no per-agent VNC screen, no 128GB Spark VM). Not MCP. Not question
widgets. Not a Mac folder picker.

Chat stays primary. Desktop gains a **320px RIGHT computer pane** (same width
language as the identity overlay, but this one is a **pane next to chat**,
not a 320px overlay). It can collapse. v0.6 defaulted open with a
chat-header control; **v0.34** (Design lock) is one 12px seam chevron,
default collapsed, last choice persisted (see §40). Sidebar stays 256px. Identity overlay (320px)
still overlays chat, not a fourth column. Do not steal transcript scroll.
Do not turn the app into a VNC client.

iOS: no computer pane this slice (chat + muted tool traces only; lag
allowed).

The pane shows the active conversation’s workspace, same root as runtime
`workspace_for()`:

- 1:1 → `~/.snorlax-bot/workspaces/agents/{agentId}/`
- Channel with `sharedProject` true → `workspaces/channels/{channelId}/`
- Channel with `sharedProject` false → first member agent’s workspace
  (speaking/selected is unclear on a GET). Do not invent a Mac path.

Empty workspace is a fine empty state (no fake files). Click a text file to
preview in the pane. Directories expand. Path jail identical to tools (no
`..`, no abs escape). Binary: show a short “binary / too large”, not a hex
dump. No terminal GUI, no screenshot stream, no take-over vs watch-only
(watch-only implied: user browses files; agents still mutate via tools).

Clients never call tools or the model. New GETs are runtime reads of the
sandbox, not a second tool loop. Isolation unchanged: the pane never shows
another 1:1’s files. B’s channel workspace is not painted inside A’s 1:1
pane.

### API (additive, camelCase `/v1`)

- `GET /v1/agents/{id}/workspace?path=` → `{ root, path, entries: [{ name, kind: file|dir, size? }] }`
- `GET /v1/agents/{id}/workspace/file?path=` → `{ path, content, truncated? }`
  404 if missing, 422 if escape / binary policy.

Auth: same bearer. 404 conversation. Path default `.`. Size cap similar to
`read_file` (256k). `root` is sandbox-relative (`workspaces/agents/{id}`),
never a host Mac path. No `projectPath`, `folderPath`, or picker. No new SSE
required; desktop may refetch after `message.done` / `tool.done`. Unknown
events ignored.

Seed `snorlax-bot-group` DELETE stays 204, no reseed. Seed PATCH identity
409 except `sharedProject`. Hop / report-back / tool loop / PR #34 follow-up
stay.

Not in v0.6: MCP marketplace UI, browser-use GUI, screenshot stream, terminal GUI,
take-over, iOS file browser, host folder picker, question widgets. Runtime MCP
client is **v0.7**.

## 13. v0.7 — MCP client (locked)

The FastAPI runtime is the MCP client. Desktop and iOS never speak MCP, never
call tools, and never call the model.

CEO locks (2026-08-26):

1. **stdio and LAN.** Connect a local stdio MCP server *and* an MCP server on
   the same LAN (HTTP/SSE or streamable HTTP). Do not assume MCP must be on
   the public internet.
2. **Config on disk** under `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR` (default `~/.snorlax-bot`),
   e.g. `mcp.json`. Not in the desktop/iOS bundle. No marketplace UI, no
   Settings picker this slice. Empty or missing file = no MCP; built-ins
   still work.
3. **Same tool loop.** MCP tools join the existing runtime-owned OpenAI loop
   (auto-run, max 8 rounds, `kind=tool` persist + `tool.start`/`tool.done`).
4. **Built-in names win.** `list_dir`, `read_file`, `write_file`,
   `delete_file`, `shell`, `web_search`, `web_fetch` are never replaced.
   Prefix MCP tools `server__tool`.
5. **Shell still has no extra network.** MCP HTTP is the runtime’s HTTP path
   (like `web_fetch`), not the agent shell.
6. **Boot and follow-up.** If an MCP server fails to start, the runtime still
   boots and built-ins work (log the failure). After a failed MCP tool, still
   leave a normal assistant follow-up (same as #34).
7. **LAN URLs.** Allow loopback, RFC1918, `.local`. Do not require a public
   hostname. Reject obvious SSRF to link-local metadata (`169.254.169.254`).

### Config

```json
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "example-stdio": { "command": "python3", "args": ["path/to/server.py"], "env": {} },
    "example-lan": { "url": "http://127.0.0.1:8765/mcp" }
  }
}
```

Optional `"transport": "sse"` (or a URL path ending in `/sse`) selects legacy
SSE; otherwise a `url` is streamable HTTP.

### Chrome

Reuse muted 12px `kind=tool` lines (`Used server__tool` / `Ran …`). No new
computer preview. iOS unchanged except it already paints `kind=tool`.

Not in v0.7: question widgets, VNC/browser GUI, Mac folder picker, marketplace
UI, public-cloud MCP requirement. Computer pane, isolation, report-back, hop
budget, seed channel DELETE 204, seed identity PATCH 409 except
`sharedProject`, and PR #34 follow-up after failed tools all stay.

## 14. v0.8 — question widgets (locked)

Runtime owns the card. Clients render only and never invent fields. Never a
second event type. Never a user-right answer bubble.

### Protocol

- Message `kind=widget`, LEFT, same speaker as the agent.
- Body on that row: `{ prompt, helpText?, options[1-6]: { label, value?
  (default label), description?, style?: default|primary|danger },
  allowCustom?, multiSelect?, dismissOnMoveOn? }` plus `widgetStatus`
  `pending` | `resolved` | `dismissed` and `widgetValues` (empty while
  pending) on the same Message. Clients render only — they must not invent
  a widget payload.
- SSE: persist, then `message.done` with the full Message, then the stream
  **ends**. No `widget.*`. Unknown events still ignorable.
- Answer is POST `{ widgetReply: { id, values?: string[], dismissed?: true } }`
  on that same transcript. Updates the **same** Message (resolved or
  dismissed). Do not insert a user-right bubble. `values` are option.value
  (or custom text if allowCustom). multiSelect: several values; else 422 if
  more than one. Unknown id / already closed: 422.
- Dismiss = decline: **do not wake the agent**. Pick: wake the agent with
  the values (continuation SSE on that POST).
- One pending widget per transcript: **409** if the model emits a second, or
  if the user POSTs normal content while pending unless `dismissOnMoveOn`
  (then auto-dismiss, no agent wake for the widget, then run the new user
  message).
- Isolation: A's 1:1 GET/SSE only user + A, including only A's widgets. B
  must not widget into A's 1:1. Channel widgets are thread-only (`replyTo`
  set); omit `kind=widget` from the timeline GET. Report-back stays a
  normal assistant `kind=message` as A — never copy B's widget rows.
- Tools stay auto-run. Widgets are not tool-approval cards and do not gate
  shell / web / MCP.
- OpenAPI **0.8.0**. Phrase `prompt` as a natural question, not a menu
  instruction.

### Chrome (desktop first, iOS match, no extra sheet)

- LEFT streak of the speaking agent (20px avatar + 13px name on streak
  start, 4px / 16px gaps). Not a bubble. Not a user-right bubble.
- Card: min 240px max 320px, 12px radius, 12px padding, 1px `--border`,
  `--bubble` fill. Prompt 14px / 1.4. Optional helpText 12px muted under it.
- Options stack, gap 6px, 8px radius, left-aligned 13px / 500. Desktop
  height 36px; iOS 44pt. default = border + hover `--bg-hover`. primary =
  accent 28% mix (same tint as user bubbles). danger = `--danger` label, no
  extra icons. Optional description 12px muted under the label. Max 6, one
  column.
- allowCustom: 36px field (iOS 44pt) under the options, placeholder
  `Or type your own`. multiSelect: 16px checkbox on the left, `Done`
  primary full-width under the list.
- Dismiss: 20×20 muted × top-right; no confirm. Composer stays on.
- Resolved: **same card**. Picked row(s) get a trailing 16px accent check;
  the rest mute to 50%. Dismissed: options disabled, 12px muted `Dismissed`
  under the prompt.
- One pending card per transcript.

Out of scope: box/VNC, skills, routines, MCP marketplace UI, computer pane
changes, Mac folder picker. Do not reintroduce `computerPane.ts` next to
`ComputerPane.tsx` (use `workspaceTree.ts`).

## 15. v0.9 — skills and cron routines (locked)

Scheduler is runtime-owned. Chrome is list + enable/pause only. No create
UI this slice.

### Runtime

- Skill = `SKILL.md` under `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR/skills/<slug>/SKILL.md`
  (default `~/.snorlax-bot/skills/<slug>/SKILL.md`) with YAML frontmatter
  `name` + `description`, then markdown body. No trigger of its own. No
  marketplace / client picker.
- One routine = one `agentId` + one skill slug. SQLite. Fields: `id`,
  `name`, `skill` (slug), `schedule`, `enabled`. Cron is 5-field,
  **Asia/Taipei** (fixed zone, not the Mac TZ). No Slack/GitHub/event
  listeners. No teach-a-task. No HTTP DELETE.
- Scheduler ticks inside the FastAPI process. If enabled, a matching
  cron minute wakes that agent with the skill body (not persisted as a
  user Message) and writes a **normal assistant Message** in that
  agent's 1:1: `role=assistant`, `senderId=A`, `kind=message`, optional
  `routineName` for the 12px kicker. Not a hop, not a widget, not a
  channel post. Isolation + report-back stand: never paint B in A's 1:1.
  Do not run while paused. Missed ticks while the runtime was down are
  skipped (no catch-up storm).
- `GET /v1/agents/{id}/routines` lists `{ id, name, skill, schedule,
  enabled }` (bare array; schedule is 5-field cron; optional
  `scheduleLabel` is a display string). Missing agent is **404**.
  `kind=channel` is **409**.
- Pause/enable is `PATCH /v1/agents/{id}/routines/{rid}` `{ enabled }`
  (resume = enable). Unknown routine is **404**. Channel is **409**.
- POST may seed a routine for tests (`{ name, skill, schedule }` → 201).
  **422** unknown skill / bad cron / channel id. Clients have no
  create/delete this slice.
- Tools still auto-run. Widgets OK if that run needs a decision (reuse
  v0.8 `kind=widget`; no second protocol). Seed channel DELETE **204**
  stands. OpenAPI **0.9.0**.
- Humanized `{skill} · {Weekdays 9:00}` is a client concern.

### Chrome (desktop + iOS, same PR)

- Agent info pane only (320px overlay / iOS sheet), under the 72px
  identity. Channel pane unchanged. Do not touch Computer pane. Do not
  reintroduce `computerPane.ts` (`workspaceTree.ts` stays).
- 12px muted header `Routines`, then rows. Row 44px, name 14px/500, 12px
  muted line `{skill} · {humanized Taipei schedule}` e.g.
  `status · Weekdays 9:00`.
- Trailing enable/pause switch, 44px hit, **LIVE on the read pane** (not
  behind the gear). Paused: name at 50% opacity. Empty: 12px muted
  `No routines yet.`
- Run lands in that agent's 1:1 as a normal LEFT bubble. If `routineName`
  is set, 12px muted kicker under the sender name. Isolation stands.
- iOS matches, no extra sheet. No New / create / edit / delete UI, no
  teach-a-task, no marketplace, no skill picker.

Out of scope: box/VNC, teach-a-task, MCP marketplace UI, event listeners,
computer pane changes, Mac folder picker, create/edit/delete routine UI.

## 16. v0.10 — MCP connect chrome (locked)

Runtime owns MCP. Clients never speak MCP or the model. One protocol:
`kind=connect` is the widget chrome family, **not** `kind=widget`.

### HTTP

- `GET /v1/plugins` → bare array `{ id, name, status: connected|needsAuth }`.
  Catalog may be empty. Runtime-global.
- Connect tap: `POST /v1/plugins/{id}/auth` → `{ authorizationUrl }` for
  the OS browser; OAuth callback hits the runtime (GET on Spark :8787,
  or POST `/v1/plugins/oauth/callback` with `code` + `state`).
- No uninstall / delete / store / search API this slice. No Add-custom UI
  (v0.7 `mcp.json` still works for hand-edited stdio/URL).

### Chat card

- Message `kind=connect` (LEFT, same speaker as the agent). Body
  `{ prompt, helpText?, pluginId }` plus `connectStatus`
  pending|connected|dismissed on that Message.
- SSE: persist, then `message.done`, stream ends. No `connect.*` event
  on emit. Unknown SSE events remain ignorable.
- Same-row reply: `connectReply { id }` or `{ dismissed: true }`. No
  user-right bubble. Dismiss does not wake and does not return a URL.
  `{ id }` stays pending until auth finishes and emits `connect.url`
  `{ url, pluginId }` then ends; the client opens the OS browser. The
  OAuth callback (GET on the runtime, or POST complete with code+state)
  PATCHes that Message to `connectStatus=connected` and continues the
  agent turn so tools auto-run (no approval widgets). If the plugin is
  already connected, `{ id }` continues this POST as hop-0.
- Sending the card ends the turn until connect or dismiss. Content while
  pending is **409**.
- Isolation matches widgets: A's 1:1 only A's cards; channel **thread
  only**, never the timeline; B never paints in A's 1:1.

### Chrome (desktop + iOS, same PR)

- Connect card: LEFT streak, 240–320px, 12px radius/padding. Prompt 14px
  e.g. `Connect Slack to read your channels.` Optional 12px muted help
  `Opens your browser to sign in.` One primary `Connect` 36px / iOS 44pt.
  Dismiss × 20×20. Composer stays. Resolved: **same card**, 12px muted
  `Connected` or `Dismissed`.
- Plugins list: **Settings only** (runtime-global), **not** the agent pane.
  12px muted `Plugins`, 44px rows, name 14px, 12px muted `Connected` /
  `Needs sign-in`. Needs-sign-in trailing `Connect` (same browser). Empty:
  `No plugins yet.` No store, no search, no uninstall. iOS Settings match,
  no extra sheet.
- Auth: desktop OS browser; iOS `ASWebAuthenticationSession`.

Out of scope: Box/VNC, marketplace, computer pane, `computerPane.ts`
(helpers stay `workspaceTree.ts`). OpenAPI **0.10.0**. Isolation,
report-back, widgets, cron, seed DELETE 204, Mac oMLX, no Mac folder
picker stand.

## 17. v0.11 — assistant markdown (locked)

Clients render only. No second protocol PR. No MCP mix-in.

### Backend

`Message.content` stays a **string**. Do **not** add `contentType`, mime,
html, or `blocks[]`. The runtime does **not** rewrite assistant text to
HTML or split one message into many. SSE `message.delta` is still text
chunks of that same string (`{ id, role: assistant, delta, senderId,
senderName, senderAvatar }`). User messages stay plain text as stored.
Keep `kind=widget` / `kind=connect` / `kind=tool` / `routineName` intact.

### Chrome (desktop + iOS, same PR)

- Assistant LEFT `kind=message` (from A): markdown, **no grey agent
  bubble**. **14px / 14pt** body, **16/14** headings, lists, bold.
  `@` mentions still overlay.
- Links: accent, hover underline (desktop), **https only**, OS browser /
  Safari. Reject `javascript:` / `data:` / `http:`.
- Inline code: **13px / 13pt** mono, **4px / 4pt** radius, accent 18% mix
  (mention-chip tint).
- Fenced code: **full turn width**, **8px / 8pt** radius,
  `--bg-elevated` (iOS elevated fill) + **1px / 1pt** border, **12px /
  12pt** muted language + **Copy**, **12px / 12pt / 1.45** mono,
  horizontal scroll.
- User-right stays **plain text** (no markdown). `https://` URLs are
  tappable. Tool / widget / Connect are **not** markdown.
- Streamed `message.delta` re-renders the same LEFT markdown in place
  (close an unclosed fence so the buffer does not flash).

### Out of scope

Mermaid diagrams, math/KaTeX, raw HTML (escape/strip; never inject
HTML from model text). No Box GUI, marketplace, event listeners, or
create/delete routine UI. Do not touch MCP connect chrome except where
shared bubble styling intersects.

OpenAPI **0.11.0**. Isolation, report-back, widgets, cron, connect
chrome, seed DELETE 204, Mac oMLX, no Mac folder picker stand. Do not
reintroduce `computerPane.ts` (helpers stay `workspaceTree.ts`).

## 18. v0.12 — MCP Add custom (locked)

Runtime owns MCP. Clients never speak MCP or the model. One impl PR
covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides this PR. OpenAPI **0.12.0**.
Do not mix full Box, teach-a-task, event listeners, or a marketplace
catalog.

### HTTP (runtime owns it)

Existing:

- `GET /v1/plugins` → bare array `{ id, name, status: connected|needsAuth }`
- `POST /v1/plugins/{id}/auth` → `{ authorizationUrl }`; unknown id 404

Add:

- `POST /v1/plugins` `{ name, transport: "stdio" | "url", command?, args?: string[], url? }` → **201** `{ id, name, status: connected|needsAuth }`.
  - stdio: `command` required (`args` optional). **422** if name or command missing.
  - url: `url` required. Allow https and http (LAN). **422** if missing/invalid.
  - Invalid transport combo **422**.
- `DELETE /v1/plugins/{id}` uninstalls (disconnect + drop from catalog). **204**. Unknown **404**. Do not auto-open a connect card. No separate disconnect endpoint.

GET `/v1/plugins` and `POST /v1/plugins/{id}/auth` stay as v0.10. `kind=connect` isolation unchanged. Keep v0.8 widgets, v0.9 routines, seed DELETE 204, content string.

Do not add store/search catalog APIs. Auth is still the OS
browser (`POST /v1/plugins/{id}/auth` → `{ authorizationUrl }`; desktop
system browser; iOS `ASWebAuthenticationSession`). `kind=connect` cards
are **unchanged**. Tools still auto-run after connect. No approval
widgets.

### Chrome (Settings only, not the agent pane)

Keep the existing Plugins list: 12px muted `Plugins`, trailing 12px `Add`
(not a plus-catalog), 44px rows, name 14px, 12px muted `Connected` /
`Needs sign-in`. Connected = status only. needsAuth keeps trailing
`Connect`. Empty still `No plugins yet.`

Add:

- Trailing **Add** opens a **320px sheet on top of Settings** (same
  family as New channel). Title `Add plugin`. Name 14px. Segmented
  `stdio` / `URL` (same control as Theme). stdio: `Command` +
  `Arguments` (placeholders `npx`, `-y package`). URL: `Server URL`
  (`https://`). Primary `Add` 36px, disabled until name + command or
  URL. Header × cancels. Save posts `POST /v1/plugins` then refreshes.
- Every row: 12px muted `Remove`. Confirm copy exactly:
  `Remove {name}? This disconnects it.` then danger `Remove` / `Cancel`.
- iOS Settings match: header 12pt `Add`, rows 44pt, 12pt muted
  `Remove` + same confirm copy. Add is a sheet on Settings (not a
  store). Primary `Add` 44pt, disabled until name + command or URL. No
  extra sheet beyond Add.

Desktop first, iOS match. Connect card chrome does not change.

### Out of scope

Full Box/VNC, teach-a-task, event listeners, marketplace catalog /
public plugin store. Isolation, report-back, widgets, markdown, cron
routines, seed DELETE 204, Mac oMLX, no Mac folder picker, and
`workspaceTree.ts` (never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`) stand.

OpenAPI **0.12.0**.

## 19. v0.13 — event listeners, webhook first (locked)

Runtime-owned. One impl PR covering runtime + desktop + iOS. Spec rides
this PR. OpenAPI **0.13.0**. Do not mix full Box/VNC, teach-a-task, or a
marketplace catalog. No second protocol PR.

### Product lock

- A routine has **either cron or a trigger, never both**. Result still
  fires as that agent's 1:1 LEFT `kind=message` as A, with `routineName`
  kicker (same as v0.9 cron).
- Isolation + report-back stand. B never paints in A's 1:1. Channel
  thread only if that's how cron already works — keep existing 1:1-as-A
  behavior.
- **Webhook first:** every event-trigger routine has a URL + **Copy** in
  the agent identity pane row. An HTTP **POST** to that URL fires the
  routine (same SKILL.md / prompt path as cron).
- Slack/GitHub listeners only if that MCP plugin is **already
  connected**. Do **not** block this slice on Slack/GitHub. No new OAuth
  / connect chrome. If a plugin isn't connected, omit those trigger types
  or 422 — webhook must still work with zero plugins.
- Chrome: muted 12px line is `Webhook` / `Weekdays 9:00` / connected
  Slack-GitHub `label`. Webhook rows: 12px muted Copy left of the
  switch, then Copied for 1.5s. Do not paint the URL. No New / edit /
  delete. No Connect CTA in the pane.
- Out: full Box/VNC, teach-a-task, marketplace. Desktop first, iOS match.

### HTTP (runtime owns it)

Existing v0.9:

- `GET /v1/agents/{id}/routines`
- `POST /v1/agents/{id}/routines`
- `PATCH /v1/agents/{id}/routines/{routineId}` (`enabled` / pause)

Extend Routine (and create) so a routine is **cron XOR trigger**:

- GET: `kind` is `cron` | `webhook` | `slack` | `github`. `schedule`
  (Asia/Taipei 5-field) is present **only** for cron. `webhookUrl` is
  present **only** for `kind=webhook` (full URL for Copy; clients must
  **not** paint it). Optional `label` for Slack/GitHub (e.g.
  `"Slack #eng"`, `"GitHub owner/repo"`). Null fields are omitted.
- cron create: existing `{ name, skill, schedule }`.
- webhook create: `{ name, skill, trigger: { type: "webhook" } }` →
  **201** with `webhookUrl`. Secret token lives in the path, not query.
  POST of an event routine without a trigger is **422**. POST with both
  `schedule` and `trigger` is **422**.
- Incoming webhook: `POST {webhookUrl}` (`/v1/hooks/{token}`). No
  Bearer; the token is in the URL. If enabled, run that skill as that
  agent and persist assistant 1:1 `senderId=A`, `routineName` set.
  Success **204**. If paused or unknown token: **404**, do not run. No
  catch-up.
- Slack/GitHub: `{ type: slack|github, label? }` is **422** unless
  `GET /v1/plugins` shows that plugin `status=connected`. GET omits
  Slack/GitHub rows when that plugin is not connected. Inbound
  Slack/GitHub delivery is not in this slice — use webhook. Do not emit
  Connect cards from the pane.

Keep list + enable/pause. Still no create/delete chrome. Clients may POST
in tests. No DELETE this slice. Channel still 409.

### Chrome (agent identity pane, 44px rows)

- 44px row unchanged: name 14px, trailing enable/pause.
- Muted 12px line is `Webhook` or the Taipei cron (`Weekdays 9:00`) or,
  if that MCP plugin is already connected, `Slack #eng` /
  `GitHub owner/repo`.
- Webhook rows only: 12px muted `Copy` **left of the switch**. Tap
  copies `webhookUrl` (not shown on the row). Then `Copied` for 1.5s.
  Do not paint the URL. Do not add a second sheet.
- Slack/GitHub rows: no Copy. No Connect CTA in the pane — Connect
  cards stay in chat / Settings. Unconnected: omit those kinds.
- Pause stays. No New / edit / delete.
- iOS match: 44pt rows, 12pt muted line, 12pt muted Copy left of the
  switch, pasteboard + Copied 1.5s. Do not paint the URL. No extra
  sheet.

### Out of scope

Full Box/VNC, teach-a-task, marketplace catalog / public plugin store.
Isolation, report-back, widgets, markdown, MCP connect + Add custom,
seed DELETE 204, Mac oMLX, no Mac folder picker, and `workspaceTree.ts`
(never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`) stand.

OpenAPI **0.13.0**.

  GETs, `image/png`). Live capture of the runtime-owned 1280×800
  framebuffer. Idle desktop still returns a 1280×800 shot (always on).
  Do not ship a fake decorative PNG for agents that have a sandbox.

- If that agent has **no sandbox**: `hasSandbox: false` and omit
  `imageUrl` (clients show `No computer yet.`).

- File-tree workspace GETs stay as they are (v0.6 Computer pane /

- Display only: default cursor, **no overlay, no Open**, no clicks.

- If that agent has **no sandbox**: **no frame**, 12px muted

- Otherwise the desktop is **always on**, even idle (still show the

- iOS: same block in the agent sheet, 16:10, 8pt radius, 1pt border,

## 21. v0.15 — Box takeover (locked)

**Desktop + runtime only.** One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. OpenAPI

### HTTP (runtime owns it)

- Existing `GET /v1/agents/{id}/computer` + Bearer PNG screenshot
  unchanged. GET may include `driving`: `user` | `agent` | `idle` when
  `hasSandbox` (omit when no sandbox, like `imageUrl`).
- `POST /v1/agents/{id}/computer/session` → **201** `{ sessionId }`
  (takeover session). Idempotent POST returns the same `sessionId`.
- `DELETE /v1/agents/{id}/computer/session` → **204** (Done).
  `DELETE …/computer/session/{sessionId}` also **204**; unknown
  `sessionId` → **404**.
- While session exists: `POST …/pointer` `{ x, y, type }` and
  `POST …/key` `{ key, type, text? }` in 1280×800 coords (**200**).
  Pointer `type`: `move` | `down` | `up` | `click`. Key `type`: `down`
  | `up` | `type`. When `type` is `type`, `text` is preferred if present.
- Agent tools that drive the sandbox (`computer_click`, `computer_key`)
  while session exists → **409**. POST `/messages` to that agent is
  also **409** (agent paused).
- Channel id → **409** on these routes.
- Missing agent 404. No session + pointer/key → 409. No sandbox + POST
  session → 404.

- Computer header: 12px muted `Computer` + trailing 12px `Open` when

  paused`, trailing primary `Done` 36px. **Esc = Done**.
- Pointer and keys map to sandbox coords (1280×800). **Composer is

- Agent does **not** run tools / drive the sandbox while the session

- Done returns to display-only; preview picks up the last frame.

**Desktop + runtime only.** One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. OpenAPI

### HTTP (runtime owns it)

- `POST /v1/agents/{id}/computer/record` → **201** `{ recording: true }`.

- `DELETE /v1/agents/{id}/computer/record` → **204** Stop. Does **not**
  write SKILL.md. Capture stays pending until save or discard (next
  record / session end discards).
- `POST /v1/agents/{id}/skills { name }` → **201** Skill `{ id, name }`
  writes `SKILL.md` under that agent's v0.9 skills dir
  (`SNORLAX_DATA_DIR/skills/<id>/SKILL.md`) from the pending capture.
  **422** empty name / no pending capture. Discard is skip this POST.
- GET computer may include `recording` (boolean) when `hasSandbox`.
  Omitted when no sandbox.
- Channel id → **409**. Missing agent 404.
- Capture records pointer/key (and enough screenshot context:
  `start.png` / `end.png` next to SKILL.md) so the skill is actually
  runnable later via v0.9 (`computer_click` / `computer_key` in the
  body). Do not ship an empty stub SKILL.md.
- Takeover session 201/204 and pointer/key routes stay (v0.15). Keep
  the v0.9 skill load path. No marketplace, no markdown editor API.

## 27. v0.21 — composer `/` skill autocomplete (locked)

Runtime + desktop + iOS. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.**
OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. **No new HTTP.** Typeahead list is the existing
`GET /v1/agents/{id}/skills` `{ id, name }`. Channel that GET stays **409**.

Out of scope: marketplace, blank **New** skill, treating **`@skill`** as
a mention. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.

### Product lock (match the existing `@` typeahead family)

**1:1 agent composer only.** Channel `/` is **plain text** — no popup,
no typeahead, no load path.

Trigger: `/` at the **start of a token** (not only at the start of the
whole message). `https://` is not a trigger.

Popup: **240px** (iOS **240pt**) above the composer, `--bg-elevated`
(iOS: same `.bar` family as `@`), **1px / 1pt** border, **8px / 8pt**
radius. Reuse the existing `@` overlay slot and keyboard — do not
invent a second typeahead system.

Rows: desktop **36px**, iOS **44pt**. Name **14px / 14pt**. **No avatar.**
Filter by **name** as they type.

↑↓ + Enter or click/tap inserts **`/name` as plain text** (not a chip,
not an `@`). Esc closes. Sending that message is what loads
**SKILL.md** — **no extra chip chrome in the transcript**.

Empty skill list **or no match**: **no popup, no empty copy**.

### Runtime (no new HTTP)

On ``POST /v1/agents/{id}/messages`` when `{id}` is a **1:1 agent** (not
a channel): scan the user text for `/` at a token start (same trigger as
typeahead). Match `/slug` or `/Name` with that agent’s `load_skills` /
`find_skill` (slug or frontmatter name, case-insensitive). On match,
inject **that** skill’s SKILL.md body into this turn’s prompt — the
named recipe, not only the catalog `skills_preamble`. Unknown `/foo`
stays plain text: **do not 422**, **do not persist extra chrome**.
Channel `snorlax-bot-group` (any `kind=channel`): `/` stays plain text —
no load path. Typeahead list remains existing `GET /v1/agents/{id}/skills`.
OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**.

Isolation, report-back, widgets, markdown, MCP, routines Add/Remove,
skill editor, iOS Record, seed DELETE 204, Mac oMLX, no Mac folder
picker, `workspaceTree.ts` (never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`) stand.

OpenAPI **0.18.0** (not bumped).

### Product lock

Cron / webhook / Copy / pause / Remove unchanged. **No Connect CTA in
the pane. No event picker. No marketplace.**

Add-routine sheet segmented control grows **only if** that MCP plugin
is `connected`: `Schedule` / `Webhook` / `Slack` / `GitHub` in **one
12px row**. Unconnected plugins **omit that segment** (do not show a
disabled Slack/GitHub tab).

- Slack: 14px field, placeholder `#eng`, 12px muted `Channel the bot is in.`
- GitHub: placeholder `owner/name`, 12px muted `One repo. No wildcards.`
- Primary `Add` 36px / iOS 44pt, disabled until name + skill + the
  field for that segment (cron / channel / repo). Webhook needs no extra
  field. × discards.
- Saved rows keep the v0.13 labels (`Slack #eng` / `GitHub owner/repo`),
  **no Copy**. Fire is still that agent’s 1:1 as A. Pause skips. iOS
  matches.

### HTTP (same POST `/v1/agents/{id}/routines`)

Cron XOR trigger. Cron `{ name, skill, schedule }` unchanged. Webhook
`{ name, skill, trigger: { type: "webhook" } }` unchanged (201 still
includes `webhookUrl`; Copy stays).

- Slack `{ name, skill, trigger: { type: "slack", channel: "#eng" } }`
  → **201** Routine `{ id, name, skill, enabled, kind: "slack",
  label: "Slack {channel}" }`. **No `webhookUrl`.** Require GET
  `/v1/plugins` shows a Slack plugin `status=connected`; else **422**.
  Empty/missing channel **422**.
- GitHub `{ type: "github", repo: "owner/name" }` → **201**
  `kind: "github"`, `label: "GitHub {repo}"`. Repo must be `owner/name`
  (one repo, no wildcards). Require GitHub plugin connected; else
  **422**. Empty/missing/wildcard repo **422**.
- Channel conversation id on this POST stays **422**. GET/PATCH/DELETE
  channel remain **409**.
- GET still **omits** slack/github rows unless that plugin is
  `connected`. PATCH `{ enabled }` pause/resume and DELETE 204
  unchanged.
- Store the match key (channel or repo) on the row (cron column) and
  the human label in `schedule_label` so GET `label` is `Slack #eng` /
  `GitHub owner/name`.
- `RoutineTrigger`: optional `channel` and `repo`; `label` is
  output-only on GET (clients do not POST label).

Fire: connected Slack/GitHub MCP events into `fire_routine_now` on that
agent’s 1:1 as A LEFT (same as webhook). Slack = messages in that stored
channel. GitHub = `pr-opened` / `pr-pushed` / `pr-merged` on that stored
repo (no event picker stored; ignore other events). Pause
(`enabled=false`) skips. Unknown plugin is **422** on create, not a
silent no-op at POST time.

## 30. v0.24 — curated plugin catalog (locked)

Runtime + desktop + iOS. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second
PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**.

Out of scope: public store, search, registry, VNC, extra channel types,
more than Slack+GitHub this slice, Connect CTA in the catalog besides
trailing Add, paid plugins, uninstall-from-catalog. Never reintroduce
`computerPane.ts`.

### Product lock

Custom Add / Connect / Remove **unchanged**. No search, no registry, no
uninstall-from-catalog. **No extra sheet** on Catalog Add.

Settings Plugins, **below the installed list**: 12px muted `Catalog`.
**Two 44px rows only** — `Slack`, `GitHub` — name 14px, trailing 12px
`Add` (iOS 12pt / 44pt; accent, same family as header Adds). Add POSTs
the baked-in transport **with no sheet**, then the row moves to the
installed list as `needsAuth` with v0.10 `Connect`. **If that plugin is
already installed, omit it from Catalog. If both are installed, hide
the Catalog header too.** Empty installed still `No plugins yet.` —
Catalog still shows underneath when nonempty. iOS Settings match.

### HTTP (runtime owns it)

- `GET /v1/plugins/catalog` (Bearer, runtime-global) → **200** JSON
  array, not wrapped. Shape `{ id, name, transport, command?, args?,
  url? }` — same fields PluginCreate needs so clients POST `/v1/plugins`
  with that body as-is (name + transport + command/args or url). **Omit
  nulls.**
- Curated in runtime code. **Exactly two entries** when neither is
  installed, in this order:
  1. Slack: id `slack`, name `Slack`
  2. GitHub: id `github`, name `GitHub`
- Bake a real stdio (or url) transport each that existing
  `POST /v1/plugins` already accepts (`transport` stdio|url). npx stdio
  is fine. Not a remote registry, not `mcp.json`, no search.
- **Omit** a catalog row when `GET /v1/plugins` already has a
  connected or needsAuth plugin whose id or name matches that kind
  (same needle as `plugin_kind_connected`: `slack` / `github` in
  id+name). If both installed → **200 `[]`**.
- Empty catalog is **200 `[]`** (clients hide the Catalog header). Do
  not 404.
- **No** `POST /catalog`. No uninstall-from-catalog. `DELETE
  /v1/plugins/{id}` unchanged.
- `POST /v1/plugins` unchanged: `{ name, transport, command?, args?,
  url? }` → **201** `{ id, name, status }`. Catalog Add is that POST.
  Then needsAuth Connect is existing `POST /v1/plugins/{id}/auth`
  (v0.10).
- `GET /v1/plugins` still `{ id, name, status }` only — do not add
  catalog fields there.

## 31. v0.25 — chat attachments (locked)

Runtime + desktop + iOS. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second
PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No `/v1/chats/` resource.
Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.

Out of scope: agent-sent attachments, video, public/unauthenticated
URLs, a second upload route, VNC, a list-endpoint `{ attachments: [] }`
wrapper. Legacy `MessageCreate.images: [{ mime, data }]` and
`GET /v1/images/{id}` stay (persist off-model).

### Product / Design lock

Composer + user-right only this slice. Agent-sent attachments wait.
Paperclip stays. Desktop also drop onto the composer (accent 1px
highlight). iOS paperclip: Photos or Files. Pending chips wrap above
the bar, 6px gap — never clip. Image pending: **56×56** thumb + 20px
×. File pending: **36px** chip, 13px name, 12px muted size, ×. Send is
on if there’s text **or any chip** (image-only / file-only both send).
Over 10MB: 12px `--danger` `Max 10MB.`, send off. Video drop/pick: same
line, `Video isn’t supported yet.` User-right: images stay **220×160**
in the bubble. Files sit above the text as 36px name chips (8px
radius, 1px border, 13px). Chip opens the Bearer URL. Channel and 1:1
both. `@` and `/` unchanged. iOS chips 44pt hit, same wrap.

### HTTP (runtime owns it)

- `POST /v1/agents/{id}/attachments` (Bearer, multipart field `file`)
  → **201** `{ id, kind, name, url, size }`. `{id}` is the same
  conversation as `POST /v1/agents/{id}/messages` (kind=agent 1:1 or
  kind=channel). Missing conversation **404**.
- `kind` is `image` for `image/*` (not video), else `file`.
- `url` is Bearer `GET /v1/attachments/{id}` on this host. 401 without
  Bearer. 404 unknown. No unauthenticated fetch.
- Over **10MB** → **422** `Max 10MB.`
- `video/*` → **422** `Video isn’t supported yet.`
- Empty file → **422** `Empty file.`
- Message POST adds optional `attachmentIds[]`. Empty `content` is ok
  if that list is non-empty (image-only / file-only send). Unknown or
  foreign ids (not created on this conversation) → **422** (do not 404
  the send). Persist on the **user** message. GET messages include
  `attachments: [{ id, kind, name, url, size }]` on **user-right
  only**; assistant rows are `[]`.
- Runtime includes them **in that turn**: image kinds as OpenAI
  `image_url` input; `text/*` files as filename + extracted text; else
  a short `user attached {name}` note. 1:1 and channel both.
- Isolation: A’s 1:1 attachments never appear in B’s; channel
  attachments stay in that channel thread.

`kind=message` from the agent paints the same chrome as user-right —
images **220×160**, files 36px name chips (8px radius, 1px border,
13px) — in the LEFT streak, **above** the markdown, left-aligned,

  over 10MB. Bind onto the `kind=message`, never the tool-trace row.
- Isolation: attachments ride the message. B’s assistant row is not
  in A’s 1:1, so B’s files are not either. Channel thread: speaking

video. Over 50MB video: 12px `--danger` `Max 50MB.`, send off.
Images/files still 10MB (`Max 10MB.`).

Bubble player **220×160**, **8px** radius, **1px** border (iOS **8pt**
/ **1pt**) on user-right AND agent LEFT, only on `kind=message`. Same
placement as images: above markdown/text, left-aligned on LEFT,
**6px** gap (iOS **6pt**). No autoplay. Poster + **24px** play;
click/tap plays inline with native controls (desktop `<video>`, iOS
AVPlayer). Not on tool lines, widgets, Connect cards, or the timeline
handoff card. Chip/player opens Bearer `GET /v1/attachments/{id}`. iOS
44pt hit. Channel and 1:1 both. `@` and `/` unchanged. Isolation: B’s
files/videos never paint in A’s 1:1. Channel thread: speaking agent
only. iOS Photos includes video. No extra sheet. No transcription, no
in-model watch.

### HTTP (runtime owns it)

- Same `POST /v1/agents/{id}/attachments` (Bearer, multipart field
  `file`) → **201** `{ id, kind, name, url, size }`. Lift the v0.25
  `video/*` 422.
- `Attachment.kind` is `image` | `file` | `video`.
  - `image/*` (not video) → `image`, max 10MB (`Max 10MB.`)
  - `video/*` or `.mp4` / `.mov` / `.m4v` / `.webm` / `.avi` / `.mkv`
    → `video`, max 50MB (`Max 50MB.`)
  - else → `file`, max 10MB (`Max 10MB.`)
- Empty file still **422** `Empty file.`
- `GET /v1/attachments/{id}` Bearer unchanged. 401 without Bearer. 404
  unknown. No unauthenticated fetch.
- `MessageCreate.attachmentIds[]` may include video ids. Empty
  `content` is ok if that list is non-empty. Unknown or foreign ids →
  **422**.
- GET attachments on any `kind=message` (user and agent), including
  `kind=video` rows. `kind=tool` / `widget` / `connect` stay `[]`.
- Runtime MUST NOT send video bytes to the model (no transcription, no
  in-model watch). `apply_to_user_content` skips video bytes; a short
  `user attached {name}` stub is fine. Clients play; the turn just
  includes the attachment.
- After a turn, runtime may bind a sandbox video path (`write_file` of
  a video under 50MB) onto that assistant `kind=message` (same store,
  Bearer GET). Skip over 50MB. Isolation rides the message. Do **not**
  add a watch-video tool.
- Legacy `MessageCreate.images: [{ mime, data }]` stay off-model.

## 34. v0.28 — watch-video tool (locked)

Runtime. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI
stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new client HTTP routes. No
`/v1/chats/` resource. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`. Desktop +
iOS idle this slice — no chrome changes, no Watch button, no second
player, no transcode UI, no camera.

Keep the v0.27 video-upload section. This slice adds an opt-in built-in
tool so the agent can describe a `kind=video` attachment as **text**.

Out of scope: VNC, public store, extra channel types, auto-inject every
video into the model turn, live camera, client transcode UI, a new
chrome surface, a new HTTP resource, a Watch button, a second player.

### Product / Design lock

No new chrome. The player stays the v0.27 **220×160** (native controls,
no autoplay) on `kind=message` only. `watch_video` is a **normal 12px
muted tool line** under that agent’s LEFT streak: **`Watched {name}`**
(failure: **`watch_video failed`**). Same `kind=tool` family as
`write_file` / `shell` / `web_fetch`. Not a second player, not on the
user-right, not a widget. Isolation stands: B’s video is not in A’s
1:1; a channel thread can watch only that channel’s attachments.

### HTTP (runtime owns it)

- No new client routes. Tool is on the existing tools list (same
  auto-run built-ins as `write_file` / `shell` / `web_fetch`).
- `watch_video` arguments: `{ attachmentId: string }` required (the id
  from `POST /v1/agents/{id}/attachments` / `message.attachments`).
- Lookup on the **current conversation only** (1:1 agent id or channel
  id).
  - unknown id or other conversation → tool result
    `Error: unknown attachment id` (**do not 422** the user POST)
  - `kind != video` (image/file) → `Error: not a video`
  - success → **plain text** tool result, not JSON. Include the
    filename (first line) and a short description. Cap ~8k chars.
    **NEVER** put raw or base64 video bytes in the tool result or the
    user turn.
- `done_summary`: success `Watched {name}`; failure
  `watch_video failed`.
- v0.27 stands: `apply_to_user_content` still skips video bytes (a
  short `user attached {name}` stub only; the stub may include
  `attachmentId` so the model can call the tool). Do **not** auto-call
  `watch_video` on every video attachment. The agent decides.
- Unknown / non-video / foreign id on **message POST `attachmentIds`**
  is still 422 (v0.25/v0.27). Tool lookup errors are visible follow-ups
  like other failed tools.

### How the runtime describes a video

Local only — do not call a cloud video API.

1. Resolve the attachment file on disk.
2. `ffprobe` for duration / size / resolution when available.
3. `ffmpeg` extracts a few frames (typical: three JPEGs across the
   timeline, ~320px wide).
4. If the local OpenAI-compat backend can take `image_url` (not the
   mock CI backend), caption those frames in a **separate** generate
   call. Frame bytes stay out of the chat-model turn; only the caption
   text is appended to the tool result.
5. If ffprobe, frames, or caption fail, still return **name + size**
   (and duration/resolution when probed). That is **success**, not
   `Error`.

Works offline on the Mac/Linux runtime (ffmpeg/ffprobe optional; mock
backend skips vision). No live camera. No client transcode.

v0.30 is composer clipboard paste. Paste fills the same pending chips
above the composer.

composer bar), paste (Cmd-V / Ctrl-V, and the clipboard `paste` event)
of image / file / video becomes pending chips — same **56×56** image
thumb + ×, **56×56** video poster + **16px** play + ×, **36px** file
chip name+size + ×.

  `kind=image`; name it `image.png` (or the clipboard type’s extension:
  jpeg→`image.jpg`, gif→`image.gif`, webp→`image.webp`).

  yet”).

  sends.
- Do not change transcript chrome, LEFT attachments, or the player.

**iOS:** Composer paste from `UIPasteboard`: images, videos, and files
become the same pending chips as the paperclip path. Plain text paste
stays in the text field. Same 10MB / 50MB danger copy. Photos/Files
paperclip unchanged.

### HTTP

No new routes, kinds, or OpenAPI fields. Upload is the existing
`POST /v1/agents/{id}/attachments`. Send is the existing
`attachmentIds[]` on the message POST.

### Copy

Desktop + iOS. Show on completed LEFT `kind=message` only (1:1 and
channel). After attachments + markdown, 6px gap, 12px muted action
row. 12px (iOS 12pt) text button `Copy`. On click, copy
`message.content` (markdown source, not rendered HTML) to the

control for 1.2s, then dismiss. Copy stays in place and clickable
(do not replace the Copy label). No toast overlay, no chat layout
jump. Speak / Regenerate unchanged. Webhook Copy still flips to
`Copied` for 1.5s. Not on `kind=tool` / `kind=widget` / `kind=connect`.

1:1, so there is nothing to copy there. Existing fenced-code Copy in

reverts; see §72).

### Regenerate

Desktop + iOS. 1:1 only. Channel has Copy, no Regenerate. 12px muted
`Regenerate` to the right of Copy (12px gap) **only** on the latest
completed LEFT `kind=message` in that 1:1. Hide it while a stream is
in flight (Thinking wave / SSE open). Hide it on older assistant
messages. Click does **not** paint a new user-right bubble. Reuse the
last user turn (content + attachmentIds + mentions). Runtime: existing
`POST /v1/agents/{id}/messages` with body `{ "regenerate": true }`
(empty/omitted content). Same SSE as send (`message.delta`,
`message.done`, `tool.*`, Thinking wave). Truncate the last assistant
turn — that `kind=message` plus trailing `kind=tool` lines of that
turn — then stream a replacement. GET afterwards must not return the
discarded text. 422 if there is no completed user→assistant turn to
replay, or if `regenerate` is combined with non-empty `content` /
`attachmentIds` / `widgetReply` / `connectReply`. 409 if a stream is
already open for that 1:1, or if the target is a channel. Composer,
paperclip, clipboard paste, IME Enter, `/` typeahead unchanged.

### Protocol

No new path. No `/v1/chats/`. Document the `regenerate` boolean on the
existing POST messages body in `protocol/openapi.yaml` but keep the
OpenAPI version **0.18.0**.

## 38. v0.32 — shell Approve (locked)

Runtime + desktop + iOS. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second
PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new path. No `/approve`
route. No `/v1/chats/` resource. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
Clients never speak MCP or the model. Mac oMLX / runtime URL unchanged.
No Mac folder picker.

Mutating `shell` no longer auto-runs. It pauses and emits a LEFT
`kind=approve` card (dedicated kind — **not** a widget fork, **not**
`kind=widget`). Body is `{ command }` plus `approveStatus: pending`.
Persist the Message, emit `message.done` with the full Message, then the
stream ends (same SSE shape as v0.8 widgets). No `approve.*` event.

Answer is on the same transcript POST as widgets/connect:
`{ approveReply: { id, approved: true } }` or `{ id, dismissed: true }`.
Dismissed is Deny (including the card ×). Do **not** create a user-right
bubble or a new user Message for the answer.

After Approve: run that command, then the existing 12px muted
`kind=tool` line (`Ran {cmd}` — same family as today's auto-run shell).
After Deny: patch the SAME card to Denied (12px muted `Denied`); do not
run; do not wake the agent.

Read-only shells auto-run with no card (existing `kind=tool`): `ls`,
`cat`, `pwd`, `git status`, `git log`, `git diff` (those exact commands,
including simple flags/paths that stay read-only). Any chain, pipe,
redirection, `&&` / `;` / `|`, or anything else is mutating and MUST
gate. `rm`, `mv`, `write`, `curl`, `python`, `chmod`, etc. gate.

Content send 409 while a card is pending (one pending approve per
transcript). Same 409 if the model emits a second approve while one is
pending. Other tools stay auto-run. Widgets / connect cards unchanged.

Isolation matches widgets: A's 1:1 only A's cards; B never paints an
approve into A's 1:1; report-back never copies a peer's card. Channel:
approve only in a thread (`replyTo` set), never the timeline.

### Chrome

LEFT card, widget/connect family: 240–320px (iOS 240–320pt), 12px/12pt
radius and padding, 1px/`--border` (iOS 1pt separator). Command is
12px/12pt / 1.45 mono, max 2 lines + ellipsis. No cwd on the card.
Desktop hover (title/tooltip) shows the full command; iOS long-press
copies the full command (no hover). Buttons stacked 6px/6pt, 10px/10pt
under the command: primary Approve (accent 28% fill, 36px desktop /
44pt iOS), danger Deny (36px / 44pt). Trailing × 20×20 = Deny.

Do **not** fork WidgetCard — dedicated `kind=approve` renderer on
desktop and iOS. No Settings chrome. No new composer UI.

### Protocol

Additive Message `kind=approve` + `MessageCreate.approveReply` is
enough. Keep OpenAPI version **0.18.0**.

Delete prompt: `Remove "{name}"?`. No helpText. Options: danger

`Removed {name}`. After Keep / ×: no delete, no tool line.

local model would not call `create_agent` / `create_channel` until
those keywords showed up. Skills living only on the seed agent is
the bug.

### Copy on create and startup backfill

Copy the two seed SKILL.md files — `teammates` (项目 / 员工 →
`create_channel` / `create_agent`) and `routines` (定时 / 提醒 →
`create_routine`, plus seeded pause/delete wording) — into each
`kind=agent` workspace at `workspaces/agents/{id}/<slug>/SKILL.md`.
Missing-file only; **never overwrite**.

Same two copies on:

- `POST /v1/agents` (UI New agent)
- the `create_agent` tool

**Startup backfill:** any existing agent whose workspace lacks them
gets them (including seed `snorlax-bot`). Channels get none.

Template source is `SNORLAX_DATA_DIR/skills/<slug>/SKILL.md`
(`SEED_TEAMMATES_MARKDOWN` / `SEED_ROUTINES_MARKDOWN` in `skills.py`).
User DELETE of that global skill is not recreated; missing source is

runtime-owned under `~/.snorlax-bot/memory/{agentId}` (markdown

transcript SQLite. Not RAM-only. Not a vector store. Each fact is one
self-contained sentence. Cap **32**; the next `remember` is

muted `kind=tool` family. **No** `kind=widget`. Empty fact →

409: a channel `remember` / `forget` writes that **speaker's**
per-agent file; inject that speaker's list on their turns. DELETE of

never overwrite): 记住 / remember → `remember`; 忘掉 / forget →
`forget`. Same mappings in `TOOLS_PREAMBLE`. Copied into each
`kind=agent` workspace on create and startup backfill (missing-file

## 45. v0.39 — user-shared memory (locked)

Runtime. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second PR.** OpenAPI
stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new path. No `/v1/memory`. No
`/v1/chats/` resource. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts` (helper
stays `workspaceTree.ts`; `ComputerPane.tsx` the React component
stays). Leave stale PRs #23 and #24 untouched. Clients never speak
MCP or the model. Mac oMLX / runtime URL unchanged. No Mac folder
picker. Desktop/iOS idle this slice (no Settings memory page, no pane
change). Creating user facts is the remember tool in chat.

v0.36 already persists per-agent facts via `remember` / `forget`
(`~/.snorlax-bot/memory/{agentId}/MEMORY.md`, cap 32, injected into
that speaker's prompt). v0.37/v0.38 list those on the agent pane.
This slice extends that same module. Do not invent a second store
protocol, a vector index, a transcript scan, or a channel-level
store.

### Tools

`remember` and `forget` gain optional `scope`: `"user"` | `"agent"`.
Omit or `"agent"` is today's behavior: write the speaking agent's
file. `"user"` writes the shared user store. Only casefold `"user"`
selects that store; any other value is `"agent"`.

User facts live at `{SNORLAX_DATA_DIR}/memory/user/MEMORY.md` (same
MEMORY.md bullet format, same `normalize_fact`, same atomic
`save_facts`). Not the sandbox. Not a channel store. Id `user` is
reserved for the human (`senderId` already is `user`); never treat it
as a roster agent (create agent/channel slug loops skip it).

Cap **32** user facts, separate from each agent's 32. Same
`MAX_FACT_CHARS` 400. Empty → `Error: missing fact`; unknown forget →
`Error: no matching fact`; too long → `Error: fact is too long`.
A full **agent** file is `Error: Memory is full`. A full **user**
file is exactly `Error: Shared memory is full.` Duplicate remember is
a no-op that still returns `Remembered`.

`forget` with `scope: user` looks only in the user store. Forget
without `scope: "user"` never touches `memory/user/MEMORY.md`.

Tool line / `done_summary`: `Remembered` / `Forgot` only. Never paint
the fact. Never paint the scope word in chrome. Auto-run. No widget.
No approve.

### Inject

Each turn injects user facts first, then that speaker's agent facts,
under the existing `### Memory` heading. Every agent (1:1 and
channel) gets the user list. B never loads A's agent file. Channel
`remember` / `forget` default still writes the speaker's agent file;
`scope: user` writes the shared file. Empty lists inject nothing.

Preamble: user facts are shared across every agent; agent facts stay
private to this agent. Do not tell the model to recite the list
unless asked. Do not invent a second `### User Memory` protocol.

### Isolation / HTTP

Agent DELETE still drops that agent's memory dir and must **never**
rmtree `memory/user/`. GET/DELETE `/v1/agents/{id}/memory` stay
agent-only (v0.37 pane). Channel ids stay 409. Do not list user
facts on the agent pane. Seed agent DELETE stays 204. Report-back /
1:1 isolation unchanged.

public store, coding-agent, Settings memory UI, Memory-sources-on-LEFT
chrome, new HTTP, OpenAPI bump.

Tap Speak → fetch/play audio for that message’s plain text

chrome beyond Speak/Stop). Tap Stop (or Speak again while
playing) stops playback. Starting Speak on another message
stops the previous one. **Never autoplay** on SSE /

## 51. v0.45 — mermaid diagrams in assistant markdown (locked)

Extends the v0.11 assistant markdown path. `Message.content`
stays a **string**. Do not add `contentType`, mime, html, or
`blocks[]`. Runtime does not rewrite or split. SSE
`message.delta` is still text chunks of that same string.

### Scope

LEFT `kind=message` only (1:1 and channel) — the same places
markdown already renders. Not tool / widget / connect / approve /
thinking / user-right.

Trigger: a fenced code block whose language tag is exactly
`mermaid` (case-insensitive if the rest of the fence chrome
already treats tags that way).

### Chrome

Valid mermaid: render an inline diagram (client-side). Desktop
uses the official mermaid library. iOS uses a safe local
equivalent (WKWebView with bundled mermaid). Never a cloud
mermaid render service.

Invalid / failed parse: fall back to the existing fenced-code
chrome (language label + Copy + mono body). Never crash the
bubble. Never blank the message.

Diagram chrome reuses the fence family (do not invent a separate
card kind):

- full-turn width
- **8px / 8pt** radius
- `--bg-elevated` + **1px / 1pt** border
- **12px / 12pt** muted language label `mermaid` + **Copy**
  (copies the source)
- horizontal scroll if the SVG is wider than the column

### Streaming

While the assistant message is still streaming, keep showing the
fence as code (defer diagram render). After `message.done` /
completed, diagrams must render.

### Speak (v0.44)

When stripping markdown for Speak, treat mermaid fences like
other fences (spoken text gets the source or is omitted
reasonably). Do not invent UI chrome. Do not narrate the
diagram.

## 52. v0.46 — math in assistant markdown (locked)

Desktop + iOS. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second

Extends the v0.11 assistant markdown path (and keeps v0.45
mermaid). `Message.content` stays a **string**. Do not add
`contentType`, mime, html, or `blocks[]`. Runtime does not
rewrite or split. SSE `message.delta` is still text chunks of
that same string.

### Scope

LEFT `kind=message` only (1:1 and channel) — the same places
markdown already renders. Not tool / widget / connect / approve /
thinking / user-right.

### Delimiters

- Inline: `\( ... \)`
- Block: `$$ ... $$` on their own lines
- Do **not** enable single-`$...$` (currency false positives)
- Optional `\[ ... \]` is **not** enabled this slice (stay
  strict to `\(`/`\)` and `$$`)

### Chrome

Valid math: render with KaTeX (or equivalent local engine).
Desktop: KaTeX. iOS: WKWebView + bundled KaTeX. Never a cloud
math service. Bundled assets are OK.

Invalid / failed parse: fall back to readable monospace source
(delimiters visible) — never crash the bubble, never blank the
message.

Chrome inherits the existing markdown column. Block math is
full-turn width with horizontal scroll if needed. Do not invent
a separate card kind. No zoom/pan chrome beyond native scroll.
Prefer app CSS variables (`--text`) over inventing new theme
pickers.

### Streaming

Defer render until delimiters are closed / the message
completes — avoid thrashing half-drawn formulas. After
`message.done` / completed, math must render.

### Speak (v0.44)

When stripping markdown for Speak, treat math like other markup
(speak plain TeX source). Do not invent UI chrome. Do not try
to narrate rendered formulas as images.

### Mermaid (v0.45)

Must keep working unchanged for fenced `mermaid` on LEFT
`kind=message`.

## 53. v0.47 — stick-to-bottom while streaming (locked)

Desktop + iOS. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second
PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP
routes. Runtime idle. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
Leave stale PRs #23 and #24 untouched. Clients never speak MCP
or the model. Mac oMLX / runtime URL unchanged.

### Stick

If the user is within **~64px / 64pt** of the bottom of the
transcript, follow the stream as tokens arrive (and as tool /
thinking chrome grows at the bottom).

### Freeze

If they scroll up farther than that slack, freeze. Never yank
them back to the bottom mid-stream.

### Send / Regenerates

On Send and Regenerates: snap to the bottom and re-arm stick
so the new turn follows.

### Jump to latest

When a new assistant LEFT `kind=message` bubble appears (or
grows) while the user is stuck, show a **12px / 12pt** muted
**Jump to latest** chip at the bottom of the chat column (not
inside a bubble; overlay the transcript). Tap/click snaps to
the bottom and re-arms. Dismiss when they scroll to the bottom
themselves (within the same ~64px slack). Idle scroll-up on a
quiet transcript does not show the chip.

### Composer

Composer focus stays after Send (desktop and iOS).

Out of scope: VNC, extra channel types, public store,
coding-agent, new surfaces, raw HTML, math/mermaid changes,
bumping OpenAPI past 0.18.0, a second PR.

last bubble of that turn**. Copy still copies the full
`content` string. Speak still speaks the full message.
Regenerate still 1:1 latest-only as v0.31.

Each short bubble uses the existing agent bubble fill
(desktop `var(--bubble)`, 14px radius; iOS secondary fill,
16pt radius). Short prose hugs the text; fences and block
math still stretch to the column. Attachments stay above the
stack. Gap between bubbles of the same turn is **4px / 4pt**.

### Stick (v0.47)

v0.47 stick-to-bottom stands: follow within ~64px while
armed; freeze on scroll-up; Send / Regenerates snap and
re-arm; Jump to latest while stuck. Completing a stream (and
the one-time split) must not yank a frozen reader.

## 55. v0.49 — optimistic user-RIGHT bubble on Send (locked)

Desktop + iOS. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second
PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP
routes. Runtime idle. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
Leave stale PRs #23 and #24 untouched. Clients never speak MCP
or the model. Mac oMLX / runtime URL unchanged.

### Send

On Send: immediately show an optimistic user-RIGHT bubble with
the outgoing **text plus any pending attachment chips** (Grok
Bot feel — chat feels instant). Clear the composer. Keep
composer focus. **Block a second Send** until that round-trip
settles (`busy` / `isSending`). Stick-to-bottom (v0.47) still
snaps and re-arms.

Pending chips are **uploaded first** (`POST
/v1/agents/{id}/attachments`) and only then painted on Send.
Send does not start while an upload is in flight.

### Success

On success: reconcile the optimistic bubble to the **server
id** of that user `kind=message` (same content). Do not paint
two RIGHT bubbles for one Send. A GET after the stream, or an
SSE `message.done` for that user row, replaces the local id in
place.

### Failure

On **4xx/5xx** (and a transport failure that never returns an
HTTP status): remove the optimistic bubble, restore the text
**and chips** to the composer, and show a muted **Couldn't
send.** hint. The hint is **12px / 12pt** muted status
(`role="status"` / equivalent), same family as
`Transcribing…` / `No speech detected.` / `Microphone is
off.` — not the red composer error line.

Widget / approve / connect replies and **Regenerates** do not
insert an optimistic user-RIGHT bubble. Regenerates stay as
v0.31 (`{ regenerate: true }`; no user-RIGHT).

### Stick / multi-bubbles

v0.47 stick-to-bottom stands. v0.48 completed LEFT
`kind=message` multi-bubbles stand. User-right stays one
plain bubble (no blank-line split).

Out of scope: VNC / full Box GUI, extra channel types, public
store, coding-agent, new surfaces, raw HTML, bumping OpenAPI
past 0.18.0, a second PR.

## 56. v0.50 — Stop generating mid-stream (locked)

Desktop + iOS. One impl PR. Spec rides this PR. **No second
PR.** OpenAPI stays **0.18.0**. Do not bump. No new HTTP
routes. Runtime idle. Never reintroduce `computerPane.ts`.
